### 2026-11-13 td.html: ป้อมแชมเปี้ยนเผ่า, a tower defense (owner, discouraged by bag.html looking "ขาดๆ เกินๆ": "ทำเกม tower defense สวยๆ มา")
- One file, no new generated art: map1 ground + props (whole map on screen, cover fit, y-sorted, props fade over pads / towers / the gate), monsters `mob_*.png` on 3 lanes (east road; north ramp from wave 3; plateau from wave 7) to a crystal gate on the west edge, towers = the champions' PixelLab clips (`art/bag/ch_<k>.png`) at 0.62 on 12 build pads, effects from `fx/`.
- Towers (3 levels, stars): rabbit 70 g (fast single punches; level 3 = every 5th attack the GPT flurry sheet, 5 hits), bear 110 (ground slam area + stun; level 3 wider, longer), fox 100 (long range fire orb, splash, burn, magic = ignores armour; level 3 three orbs). Upgrade 0.9x / 1.5x of the price, sell 60%.
- 15 waves, golem boss on 5 / 10 / 15, 20 lives (boss leak = 5), speed x1-x3, full-screen button, title + end cards, best wave in localStorage `td.best`. `window.TD` (`G`, `build`, `upgrade`, `startWave`, `step`, `bot()`); `?skip=1` skips the title.
- Balance by `TD.bot()` (fixed build order, upgrades lowest first): wins wave 15 with 2-10 lives left; not human-tested. Ideas not built: more champions as towers when their sheets come, map2 as a second stage, hero skill button, sound.
- Owner after the first version: "แค่นี้หรือ" → second batch, still free: stage select with a 2nd stage on map2 (`STAGES`: ป่าใบไม้แดง, 2 lanes meeting before the bridge, monsters x1.25 hp, `?stage=2`), two player skills (☄ meteor: tap the button then a spot, area magic damage 70 + 16 x wave + burn, 22 s; ❄ freeze: every monster stunned 3.5 s, bosses 1.5 s, 38 s), sound made in code (WebAudio, mute button, `td.mute`), a boss bar at the top, best wave per stage (`td.best0/1`). Bot (no skills): stage 1 wins with ~10 lives, stage 2 wins or dies at wave 10.
- Owner: "ไม่มีสู้กับคนอื่นหรือ แปลกใหม่หน่อย" → duel mode in td.html (free). Each side defends its own copy of the map; no waves: creeps come by themselves and grow with time (`duelStep`, wave = 1 + t / 24 s), gold arrives every 10 s (income, starts 20), kills pay 60%. The new part: a row of 6 monster buttons (`SEND`: cost, income gained) sends that monster to the OTHER side and raises your income for good. First gate to 0 lives loses.
  - vs bot: a second board `BOT` runs the same `step` (global `G` is swapped; `hud` / `say` / `snd` only act for `ME`), `botAI` builds by `ST.bot`, upgrades and sends. A player who only defends beat it in ~5 min in the fast sim.
  - online: PeerJS from unpkg (browser to browser, public broker), 4-digit room code (`hostRoom` / `joinRoom`, peer id `lastsignal-td-<code>`). Only `{t:'start',stage}`, `{t:'send',k}`, `{t:'lives',n}` travel; leaving = the other wins. Protocol tested with a mocked Peer between two pages (start, send, lose / win); the REAL connection could not be tested from the cloud session (unpkg is blocked there): the owner must try it with two devices.

- `MAP=td3 python3 tools/art/td_map.py` -> `art/td/map3/` + `art/td/map3_preview.png`: a cold ruined graveyard east of map 2. Only art already in the repo, recoloured in code (grey dirt / dead grass / paved road from the path_pro + cliff tiles, withered edge trees, the dead tree `trees_autumn_3`, ruins, headstones = `ruins_8` in rows, rocks, a murky pool, two plateaus with stairs). Everything inside is hand-placed in the `if T3:` block.

Map 2's ground.png / props.png are byte-identical (td.html uses them); only its block.png (the border opened at the exit) and map.json (exits / entries) changed. Map 1 is untouched.

Hero sheets still come from `art/td/map1/`.

### 2026-11-13 the ground of the three maps repainted in the look of `art/incoming/mapgen/scene1.png` (owner: "ทำแมพให้สวย")
- Free, code only (`tools/art/td_map.py`, section "ground painter"): no PixelLab job. `art/td/map1|2|3/ground.png` + the three `map*_preview.png` are new; `block.png`, `props.png`, `map.json` and the mob sheets come out byte-identical to before (checked against git), so collision, props, exits and the hard-coded road / pad coordinates of `td.html` are untouched. Before / after for the owner: `art/td/ground_before_after.png` (same 900x550 region of each map, props on both).
- What changed in the look: the old ground was 32 px texture tiles + noise + Bayer dithering + an olive grade (reads as static). Now it is flat colour areas in a small palette (about 70-110 colours a map instead of 1,000-2,400), hard steps, painted at half size and doubled (one art pixel = 2x2 map pixels = the grid of `block.png`, the same chunkiness as the reference next to the trees). Road: light inside, darker band, dark lip under the grass, a dark-green toothed grass edge, oval marks, pebbles, tufts over the lower edge. Shadows: bold flat shapes under every prop (same ellipses as before) and at the foot of the walls. Small life: daisies in clusters, tufts, dark grass clumps, small stones. Water: blue in two tones, light line along a dark bank, ripple strokes, a few small lily pads, a ring round the lily-pad / boat props. Plateau walls are rock columns with grey rim stones, the stairs have 6 px steps.
- Map 2 keeps autumn: orange / red / yellow leaf carpets (with leaf shapes on them) where they were, green patches, cream flowers, a few tiny mushrooms, a repainted plank bridge. Map 3 keeps the cold graveyard: blue-grey dirt, broken flagstones, grey-green dead grass, the paved road as slabs in running bond with worn spots, a murky pool; no daisies, only dry tufts, stones and cracks.
- The big light / dark patches, leaf carpets and rock / dead-grass patches lie where the old map had them (same noise fields). The script still makes the old random draws for the texture fill (now unused) so the props land where they always did; the painter has its own generators (`RG`, `RR`). Do not put new `random.*` / `np.random.*` calls before the props.
- Checked in the pages (Playwright, 844x390, DSF 2): `map.html?hero=dagger&px=1&map=td1|td2|td3` walking right / up, `td.html?skip=1` and `&stage=2` (build pads still beside the roads): no page errors, no failed requests.
- Short of the reference, honestly: patch shapes are noise blobs, not hand-placed; the road is narrower than in the picture (geometry is fixed) so it holds fewer marks; grass teeth are small; trees / rocks are the old props in their olive grade with finer pixels than the ground, and the lily-pad props still carry their grey disc of old water. A true match needs props redrawn in the same chunky style (that would be PixelLab / GPT work: only on the owner's order).
- Same job, in the page: `map.html` `ambience()` (free): three slow cloud shadows, a soft dark screen edge, and per map pollen lights (td1), falling leaves (td2), cold mist + pale motes (td3), world-anchored; `?amb=0` turns it off. Shipped together with the ground repaint.

### 2026-11-13 map.html becomes a grinding loop (owner: "ทำเกมส์เก็บเวลต่อ"; free, code only, no PixelLab job)
- Save: level, XP, kills, gold, upgrades in `localStorage` key `ls_grind1` (shared by both heroes; kept across maps, the hero swap and a reload; `?new=1` or "เริ่มใหม่ทั้งหมด" in the shop starts over). Saved on every kill / purchase / page hide.
- Drops: every monster drops gold coins (total = max HP / 10, elite x2) that fly to the hero within 120 px; 10% drop a heart (+25% HP; elite 50%, boss 3). HP also comes back 3% a second after 5 s without being hit.
- Elites: 12% of spawns (rolled again on each respawn): 1.25x size, 2.5x HP, 1.5x damage, faster, gold ring at the feet, 1.5x XP on top of the HP.
- Upgrades (🛒 button / U, pauses the game): attack +10%, max HP +25, crit +4% (x2 damage, base 5%, max 10 steps); cost 30 x 1.55^step. Plain stat steps on purpose: NOT a build / loadout system.
- Boss per map: after 12 kills on the map (15 for the next ones; counter "บอส n/12" under the bars) the boss appears near the hero: td1 จ่าฝูงหมูป่า (boar_guard x1.7, 600 HP, areas hit 18), td2 โกเลมใบไม้แดง (golem x1.6, 1,400, 28), td3 ราชาสุสานหิน (golem x1.9, 3,200, 45). Attacks are RED GROUND WARNINGS (`HZ`): one area under the hero → three small ones → one → a big burst round the boss; an inner disc grows until it goes off; under 40% HP everything is 20% faster. Rolling / blinking through works (invulnerable). HP bar at the top of the screen; leaving the map drops the fight.
- Also: cooldown sweep on the two skill buttons; crit numbers are big / gold.
- Checked with Playwright (844x390), both heroes: kills → gold + XP + level, boss appears at 12, areas hurt a standing hero, boss dies → gold / hearts / counter back to 0, shop buys, values survive a reload on another map; no page errors. NOT tried on a real phone. Balance is a first guess (boss HP, prices): tune after the owner plays.
- Next ideas, not built: ranged monsters (goblin archer shoots), ground-targeted hero skills with the big VFX sheets, monster levels per zone, equipment drops.

### 2026-11-13 (late night) `gem.html` = ศึกอัญมณี, a match-3 battle (owner: "สร้างเกมเรียงเพชรแบบต้องสู้ เรียงเพชรแล้วกลายเป็นการโจมตี เอาสวยๆ สุดมันๆ ไม่จำกัด เอาที่ Claude ถนัดที่สุด")
- **No PixelLab job, 0 credits, no new generated art.** "ไม่จำกัด" was read as freedom of design, not as an order to generate (the hard rule stays: only on "เจน" / "ทำเลย"). What Claude is best at = code, so everything new is drawn / animated / synthesised in code on top of art the repo already owns: champions `art/bag/ch_<k>.png` + `sk_rabbit_gpt.png`, monsters `art/action/mobs_hr/` (42 sheets: walk / attack / death / hurt), arena `art/bag/arena.png`, pixel effects `art/pvfx/` (PVFX Foundry, CC0). `map.html`, `bag.html`, `td.html` are untouched.
- **The game**: the party is the three champions (rabbit, bear, fox; shared HP). A 7x7 board of six gems, swap two neighbours (drag, or tap then tap) to line up 3+. Every colour is one action and the cleared gems fly off as orbs to whoever acts: white diamond = rabbit punches the targeted monster (9 a gem), yellow topaz = bear slams the ground, all monsters (5.5 each), red ruby = fox fireball + burn for 2 turns (7.5, burn 25% a turn), green emerald = heal (4.5 x HP ratio), blue sapphire = shield (5, cap 35% of max HP), purple amethyst = ultimate charge for all three (7; own colour gives its champion 5). A falling cascade is a combo: power x(1 + 0.25 per extra step, cap x3); bigger clears count more (x1.1 per gem over 3, cap x2). 4 in a row = line gem (clears its row / column), L or T = bomb gem (3x3), 5 = prism (swap it with a colour: every gem of that colour goes); line + line = cross, bomb + line = 3 rows + 3 columns, bomb + bomb = 5x5, prism + prism = the whole board.
- **Monsters** come in 3 waves a stage and count down in player moves (the number on the badge; red = attacks after the next move). Tap a monster to target it. Traits: poison (3 turns, goes through the shield), lock (chains 2 gems: they still match but cannot be moved), stone (turns 2 gems to rock: broken by a match next to them or a blast), heal (mends an ally). Bosses (stage 5 of a chapter): every third action they charge for 2 turns behind a yellow guard bar (11% of their HP): break it and they are stunned 2 turns, else a x2 hit + 3 locks + 2 rocks.
- **Ultimates** (tap a full ring, key 1 / 2 / 3; no turn used): rabbit หมัดดาวตก (5 quick hits + an uppercut = 14.5 gems on one target, the GPT skill clip), bear ค้อนถล่มปฐพี (9 gems on everyone + stun 1 turn), fox ฝนอุกกาบาต (7 gems on everyone + a strong burn).
- **Grinding**: 6 chapters x 5 stages (forest, Siam, China, Japan, Egypt, the North; the arena is colour-graded per chapter; monsters from the `ROSTER`-style lists in `CH`). Gold + XP per stage (a lost stage still pays half the gold and a part of the XP), party level = +8% attack and +34 HP, gold upgrades the six gems (+15% a step, ultimate charge +8%; cost 50 x 1.5^step), 1-3 stars by HP left. Save = `localStorage` key `ls_gem1` (`?new=1` starts a throw-away fresh save: do NOT give the owner a link with it, it overwrites the real one on the first save).
- **Looks, all code**: gems are pixel art made at run time from facet lists (`GEMDEF`, `gemPixels`: one sample per pixel, 6-colour ramps, outline, rim light, seams; the size follows the screen so a gem is always about 46 art pixels), the board panel, lights (one cached glow sprite per colour, drawn on the small scene canvas), particles, orbs, damage numbers, banners; sounds and the music loop are WebAudio synthesis (no files). The scene is a small pixel canvas blown up by K in half steps (K = 2 on a DPR 3 phone, 1.5 on DPR 2); landscape = scene left, board right; portrait = scene top, board bottom; menus are HTML and grow on a PC (`--z`).
- **Balance** (2026-11-14: these runs counted every reward twice, see the next section; the rewards were doubled to match) (table `BAL` in `gem.html`, tuned with the auto-player, not by a person yet): monster HP = table x 2.3 x (1 + 0.3 g)^0.8 x ramp(0.5), attack = table x 1.65 x (1 + 0.3 g)^0.7 x ramp(0.6), g = stage number 0-29, ramp(d) = 1 + d (1 - e^(-g/12)). (1 + 0.3 g)^0.8 is the party's own growth when every stage is cleared once and all gold is spent, so the ramp is the real difficulty: gentle at the start, a little grinding wanted later. `node tools/qa/gem_balance.js <seed> <skill>` (skill 0.85): chapter 1 at the first try (HP left 20-100%), later chapters 1-4 tries, a whole campaign 52-69 runs for 30 stages, the same for skill 0.6 and 1.0 (luck weighs more than the picker). Lessons of the tuning: the first table was far too weak (a level-1 party with no upgrades cleared chapter 3), and plain exponential growth (1.15^g) is the wrong shape for this economy: upgrades are cheap early, so the party's growth is front-loaded.
- **Checked** (Playwright; phone 844x390 at DPR 2 and 3, portrait 390x844, PC 1600x900): the page's own auto-player through all 30 stages, no page error and no stuck state; `node tools/qa/gem_fuzz.js` (36 stages of random moves with special gems / prisms / locks / rocks thrown in and random ultimates, sound on): clean; a run with real mouse actions: first-time how-to, drag swap, tap-tap swap, a wrong swap comes back and costs no turn, target tap, ultimate tap, pause, quit, a stage won by clicks, upgrade bought from the result screen, next stage, save kept after reload. One real bug found that way and fixed: the upgrade panel opened from the result screen sat behind it. Frames looked at for every state (pictures: `art/incoming/gem/shot_*.jpg`).
- **Not checked / honest limits**: not played on a real phone, sound not heard by anyone (it runs without errors, how it sounds is unknown), frame time only measured in the headless browser (software drawing: 8-13 ms a frame; a phone GPU should be far below). Balance is the auto-player's, a person may find it easier or harder. Only one arena picture (graded in six colours). The champions' attack clips face right only, which is all this game needs.
- **Next ideas, not built**: more champions as they arrive (wolf, cat, eagle, turtle, boar, deer: one gem colour each, swap the party), monster weaknesses by colour, an endless tower after 6-5, equipment drops, daily stage.

### 2026-11-14 `gem.html` rebuilt as a phone game (owner: "ย้ำนะ สวยๆ ให้เหมือนเกมในมือถือ ไม่เอาเหมือนเว็บ · นอนแล้วไม่ต้องถามนะ ทำไปเลย · อะไรขาดเจนได้เลย ให้งบ 100")
- **What "like a web page" was**: the menus of the first version were HTML boxes over the canvas (system font, rounded CSS panels, emoji as icons). Now there is NO HTML UI at all: the page is one canvas and every screen is drawn by the game, with a pixel UI kit painted in code (`pPanel`, `pButton`, `pRibbon`, `pNode`, `pRing`, `pCard`, `pPill`, `pxBar`, stars, coins, small bitmap icons in `BMP`). One art pixel of the kit = `PX` device pixels (about one CSS pixel), every piece is painted once into a cache (`kit()`), buttons sink when pressed, have a click sound and a short vibration, the green ones get a moving shine.
- **Screens** (state `SCR`, dialogs `DLG`, a fade between screens `go()`): loading bar → title (emblem + gold logo, "แตะเพื่อเริ่ม"; on a touch phone the first tap also asks for full screen + landscape) → lobby = the heroes' camp with the three champions by the fire (tap one = his card), big ผจญภัย button, อัปเกรด, วิธีเล่น, the star chest, level / XP bar, gold, settings → map = the chapter's own arena with its monsters waiting in it (shadows until the chapter is open, the boss until he is beaten), five stage medals on a dotted path with stars, arrows between chapters → stage briefing (monsters with their traits, recommended level, rewards) → fight → result (ribbon, stars popping one by one, gold and XP counting up, level-up) → next stage / upgrades / map. Settings: sound, music, vibration, full screen, landscape / portrait. A first fight opens the 3-page how-to once.
- **New in the game itself**: star chest (every 15 stars: 150, 300 … 900 gold; `S.chest`), recommended level per stage (`recLevel`, red when the party is 2+ levels under), vibration (`S.vib`), the gold counter rolls, announcements are ribbons, the monster bars step aside while a ribbon shows, portraits sit in gold rings, combo text and all numbers in the game font. Text everywhere = Kanit 600 / 800 (SIL OFL; `art/gem/font/`, notice `vendor/kanit/`).
- **Generated for it** (PixelLab pixen standard, 1 each, 16 of the 100; log rows at the top of this file): five arenas (Siam temple, Chinese tomb, Japanese shrine, Egyptian court, Norse fjord) so every chapter has its own place instead of one arena tinted six ways, the camp for the lobby, nine 32 px icons, the emblem. Raw in `art/incoming/gem/raw/`, in use in `art/gem/` via `tools/art/gem_assets.py` (copies only). Table `ARENA` in `gem.html` holds per arena: floor line `fy`, lights that flicker (`L`), still glows (`G`), mist colour; the camp fire throws embers (`E`). Sounds and music are still synthesised: PixelLab's music / sound tools say they need the tier 2 plan (not tried, the account is tier 1), and nobody could have listened to the result anyway.
- **Home-screen app**: `gem.webmanifest` + icons `art/gem/app192.png` / `app512.png` (the emblem): "add to home screen" should open it full screen in landscape without the browser bar. NOT tried on a real phone. No service worker on purpose (it would keep old versions around; the owner already fights the browser cache).
- **Balance bug found and fixed**: yesterday's tuning counted every reward TWICE. `simStage` let the page's own result screen pay (`grant`) and then the test script paid again, so the auto-player levelled twice as fast as a person would: the game that shipped on 2026-11-13 was about twice as grindy as the note above says (the same auto-player with single pay: 117 runs for the campaign, 4 tries at 1-5). Fix: `simStage` no longer pays, and the real rewards are doubled so a player gets exactly what was tuned (`BAL.xp` 22 → 44, kill gold (10 + 0.2 x table HP) x 1.07^g, stage gold 30 + 12 g; first-clear bonus 30 + 10 g unchanged). After the fix, one campaign of the auto-player at skill 0.85 / 0.6 / 1.0: 63-72 runs, chapter 1 at the first or second try, later chapters 1-4 tries. A first win now gives a level-up at once. The star chest gold comes on top (not in the simulation: slightly easier).
- A seed does not repeat a run exactly: effect timings use `Math.random` and decide which hit lands first. Balance numbers are bands, not fixed values.
- **Checked** (Playwright, ONE browser at a time): every screen and dialog looked at in landscape phone 844x390 (DPR 1, 2, 3) and 740x360, portrait 390x844, PC 1600x900; `node tools/qa/gem_play.js` = a full run with real clicks and drags (title → lobby → map → locked stage does nothing → briefing → first-time how-to → drag swap, tap-tap swap, wrong swap, target, ultimate, pause, quit → a stage won by drags → result → upgrade bought from it → next stage → save kept after reload): no errors; `gem_fuzz.js` 36 stages: no errors, nothing stuck; draw time in the headless browser 3-11 ms a frame on a DPR 3 phone size. A battle in each of the six arenas looked at (fighters stand on the floor line).
- **Not checked**: a real phone (touch, full screen, vibration, the home-screen install), the sound by ear, how the generated arenas read on a small screen in daylight. The run that started four browsers at once killed the cloud session's worker (out of memory); nothing was lost, but run the checks one by one.
- Pictures: `art/incoming/gem/shot_*.jpg` (title, lobby, map, briefing, battle, ultimate, result, upgrades, portrait).
- **Next ideas, not built**: more champions when their sheets arrive (the lobby has room for a roster), a daily stage, monster weaknesses by colour, an endless tower after 6-5, real recorded sound if the plan allows.

### 2026-11-14 owner: start over on Roblox ("ทำใหม่เลย")
- Owner asked whether Roblox would be easier (GPT told them Claude can connect to Roblox + Blender by MCP), then decided to start over there. Facts given: Roblox Studio has its own built-in MCP server (Assistant → Manage MCP Servers, quick connect for Claude Desktop / Claude Code); a Blender MCP exists (community-made). Both run on the owner's Windows PC, so the work must be done from Claude Desktop / Claude Code there, not from a cloud session.
- Moves on Roblox: the 2D pixel clips (PixelLab, GPT sheets) do not carry over. Plan told to the owner: build the champions as R15 rigs (two legs, two arms, animal heads / ears / tails as parts) so that (1) Roblox's own walk / run / jump / idle / climb / swim come free, (2) bought or free animation packs and Mixamo moves (retargeted in Blender) fit, (3) attacks and skills are keyed in Studio's Animation Editor or captured from a video of a person, and big effects come from Roblox particles / beams. Claude can write the scripts that play moves on hits and chain combos; Claude keying poses by script is possible but stiff, not the way for pretty attacks.
- The web games (`gem.html`, `map.html`, `bag.html`, `td.html`) stay as they are.

### 2026-11-14 blue hit burst from Meta AI (owner: "เก็บไว้ใช้ได้ไหม" → made with Meta)
- Owner's own Meta AI GIF (720x720, 13 frames at 40 ms): a blue slash flash, then a cel-shaded blue puff burst that cools to dark purple smoke and falls apart. Raw kept: `art/incoming/fx_meta/blue_slash_raw.gif`.
- Cut free by `tools/art/meta_fx_cut.py` (background = median of the near-empty frames 0, 9-12; real alpha, because the late smoke is DARKER than the grey grid, so an additive cut would lose it): `fx/meta_blueburst.png`, 13 cells of 617x589, key `meta_blueburst` in `fx/manifest.json` (25 fps, anchor in the middle, normal alpha: draw it with source-over, not lighter). Nothing of the effect was removed. Small limits: the soft glow round the first-frame slash turns into a thin dark rim on light grounds, and a faint trace of the grid shows in frame 7 on light grounds; on the game's dark scenes neither shows.

- 2026-11-14 later (owner: "ทำเรียงเพชรต่อ"): the burst is IN `gem.html`. Drawn smooth at screen size (it is a painted effect, not pixel art), list `DFX`, `blueBurst()` / `drawBursts()`: (1) over the target of every heavy or critical hit that is not fire (fox hits and burns keep their own orange effects; the rabbit ultimate's last uppercut ends in it), size 1.5x the monster's height (1.1x for a boss); (2) over a bomb gem when it goes off, size of its blast, kept inside the board's frame. From frame 5 on the smoke fades to 80% and out, so the gems falling behind it stay readable. Checked: frames looked at, `gem_fuzz.js` and `gem_play.js` clean.

Fixed the same day: the swords are drawn blade up to the LEFT, handle bottom right (not the other way), so grip = the opaque pixel nearest the bottom-right corner; all 180 now measure 38-44 px grip to tip. Preview `art/incoming/swords/preview.png`.
- Plan talked through: for swords pinned to the hand, characters must be small, about 32-48 px tall (chibi); our 128 px heroes would make them look like toothpicks. One bare-handed body + a hand point and angle per frame, the sword drawn at the hand, turned in 45° steps or pre-rotated (pixel art turns badly at odd angles). Not built: waiting for a small bare-handed character (GPT from the pose sheets, free, or PixelLab about 1 credit on "เจน").

- Swords-in-hand test (same day, free code): the small rabbit's side view + 5 swords pinned at the gauntlet (hand point 27,30 in the 50x46 side cell, by eye), each turned about its grip to 5 angles of a swing (wind-up behind, up, forward-up, forward, down) with nearest-neighbour rotation: `art/incoming/smallchamp/pin_test.png`, `swing_test.gif`. It works: grip lands in the hand, every sword fits the body size. Pixel jaggies at 45° are small at this size. Next for a real version: a small body with idle / run / slash frames (the shared pose library + GPT), a hand point + angle per frame, then the game draws whichever sword is equipped.

## Tribe game, first playable (owner gave 100 credits, 27 used) — `tribe.html`
Stone-age tribe on map 1 (direction v3 of `docs/fort-design.md`). IDLE village: villagers (STR / AGI / INT / VIT) pick jobs by utility (priority x need / distance, blurred by low INT; low INT takes a random node of the nearest few), gather berries / wood / stone, carry home to the fire, eat, are born as children when food + hut room allow (stats from two parents), grow up in 60 s; the village planner queues huts / spear towers / fences and villagers build them (`plan()`); the champion is only ordered (สั่งแชมป์ + tap ground), has levels, a ground slam, respawns after 40 s + 6 s/level. STAGES: READY -> waves along roads checked on the flow field (`ROADS`), monsters go for people nearby or the most exposed building, else the fire; fire at 0 = stage lost (fire relit at 30 %). Hurt villagers retreat by INT and bleed by STR on the way. Save every 10 s (`tribe_save1`), offline gains at half the recent gathering rate (max 8 h). `?new=1` starts over.
Movement: flow fields on an 8 px grid over map 1's block.png, cached per target; stuck monsters go back to a road after 4 s.
Checks: headless idle 120 s + stage 1 (no errors); balance run (90 s idle between stages): wins stages 1-7, stage 8 wipes the village. Open: resources pile up (no sinks yet: upgrades, tools, research), huts / towers have one level, no sound, no fence blocking yet (fences are just targets).
Files: `tribe.html`, `art/tribe/` (+ `orig/`), `art/tribe/map1_nodes.json` (`tools/art/tribe_nodes.py`), `tools/art/tribe_sheets.py`, shots `art/incoming/tribe/tribe_shot_*.png`.

## Side game: grinding added (owner 2026-11-14 "กลับไปทำเมเปิ้ลต่อ", free)
`side.html?hero=rabbit` now has monsters to fight: 14 placed on the ground and platforms (`HOMES`: shroom Lv1, wolf 2, goblin 3, skeleton 4, boar guard 6; sheets from `art/action/mobs`, punched copies in `art/sidegame/mobs/`), they patrol their own platform, chase and attack when the hero is close, bump damage, respawn 7 s after dying. The combo's impact frames call `doHit(n)` with a reach per hit (`REACH`: rising slash in front, spin both sides, jump slam wide), damage x1 / 1.3 / 2.3, 18 % crits, knock-back. Maple-style damage numbers (orange, pink crits, violet when hurt), hero HP / EXP bars and level at the bottom (`drawHud`, drawn at full screen size so text is sharp), level up = full HP; dying = respawn at the start, -10 % EXP. The dagger girl no longer stands in the rabbit's map.
Bot check: 3 minutes of auto-grinding -> Lv 5, never died, no errors. Open: drops (coins / items), skills, more maps, sound.

## HOODFALL: the side game made to look like a game (owner 2026-11-14 "ทำมาดูทำไมไม่เป็นเกมเลย", free)
New page `hoodfall.html` (side.html stays as the test bench). Built from the map plan `docs/side/map1_plan.md`:
title screen (HOODFALL, the map panning behind, tap to start), bright golden-hour map "ป่าตะวันรอน" 3200 x 1100 with 5 zones
(camp / mushroom meadow / wolf hill / ruins / boar den), stepped solid ground (`GROUND`, steps are walls), 12 one-way platforms, 5 climbing
ropes (up / down, jump off), side-view props (punched copies in `art/sidegame/props/`), parallax mountains, sun + light rays + pollen,
foreground bushes, code-drawn campfire (heals), chest at the top of the ruins (coins), portals (map 2 "coming soon").
23 monsters patrolling their own stretch + the boss "ราชาหมูป่า" (boar guard at x2, 2400 HP, red ground warning then slam).
Hero: rabbit run + the 3-sheet combo + skill "ทุบ" (K / 💥, 6 s cooldown, the slam hit wider and x1.5). Hit flash (white silhouettes), hit-stop,
death puffs, landing dust, coin drops with magnet, level-up burst. HUD drawn at full size in Kanit: minimap with zone name, status bar
(portrait, Lv, HP, EXP %, coins), boss bar, damage numbers; pixel-framed buttons (◀ ▶ ▲, ⚔, ⤒, 💥, 🔊). Synth sounds (swing, hit, crit,
coin, level, hurt, jump, slam, chest, warning) + a soft looping tune. Save (`hoodfall1`): level, EXP, coins, HP. `?new=1` fresh, `?play=1` skips the title.
Checks: headless title + play + 4-minute bot run (Lv 4, 280+ coins), boss area, no errors. Shots `art/incoming/hood/hoodfall_*.png`.

## Stone-age kingdom map — `kingdom.html` (owner 2026-11-15 "แผนที่ใหญ่ๆ สำหรับสร้างอาณาจักร", starter set 40 credits)
80 x 80 isometric map generated in code (seeded value noise: 2 hill levels of stacked thick tiles, lakes, a river, sand shores, stone, dirt, forests of trees / bushes / rocks), a village in the middle (cave, 3 bone huts, leaf hut, totem, 2 hide racks, a code-drawn campfire). Villagers pick the resource the tribe has least of, walk there on grid BFS paths (one level up / down), gather, carry it home; food is eaten, extra food + room = new villagers. Build bar: bone hut 30 wood + 10 stone (+3 people), leaf hut 15 wood (+2), totem, hide rack; tap a building then a tile (green / red footprint preview). Drag to pan, pinch / wheel / + − to zoom in whole steps, minimap top-left (tap to jump). `?seed=N` for another map.
Check: headless 60 s, villagers gather (wood 320 / stone 150 / food 127, 14 people), no errors. Shots `art/incoming/kingdom/kingdom_shot_*.png`.
Open: redo campfire / fence / firewood / fern tree (4 credits) or clean them (owner's call: never strip drawn parts without asking), building levels, more building types, save, roads, seasons / threats.
- Owner 2026-11-15 "โทนไม่เหมือนที่ให้ทำคลาวก่อนสี คม" (the map did not look like the stone-age scene: colours, crispness). Free fix: the ground is now PAINTED IN CODE in the target scene's colours ("ground painter" in `kingdom.html`): flat lime grass with a few tufts, sandy paths with soft wobbly edges and a shadow under the grass, grey flagstones, brown rock-slab cliffs with grass hanging over, teal water with white foam, and the whole map is an island whose outer edge drops 3 levels into the sea. Ray-cast per pixel in 256 px chunks, cached (about 23 ms a chunk on a PC). The PixelLab tiles `art/kingdom/t_*.png` are kept but no longer drawn. Campfire drawn in code (stone ring, logs, flame) in the middle of the plaza, totem moved beside it. Tap-to-cell was half a cell off (fixed). Compare `art/incoming/kingdom/kingdom_v2_compare.png`.
- Still not like the target: trees, bushes, rocks, cave and leaf hut stand on the small raised grass slab PixelLab drew under them (darker olive) — cutting those slabs off needs the owner's OK (rule: never strip drawn parts without asking); or regenerate them without a slab (paid, only on "เจน").

## Stats + runes in hoodfall (owner 2026-11-15 "ทำระบบ สเตตัส รูน", free)
- Button "สเตตัส" top right (red badge = unspent points), keys C (stats) / I (runes), Esc closes; the world pauses while it is open. HTML panel in the pad style (gold frame, dark brown).
- STATS: 3 points per level (old saves get (Lv-1) x 3). แม่นธนู DEX +2 attack · ว่องไว AGI +1% attack speed (archer move playback), +0.4% move speed · โชค LUK +0.5% crit, +1% coins · อึด VIT +12 max HP. Base: attack 12 + 4/Lv, crit 15%, HP 120 + 25/Lv. Caps: crit 60%, attack speed 160%, cooldown -50%.
- RUNES: drop 7% from monsters (boss always, rare 70% / legend 30%), normal drop rarity 80 / 18 / 2 %. 6 kinds x 3 rarities (ธรรมดา / หายาก / ตำนาน): เพลิง attack +6/12/20% · วายุ attack speed +5/9/15% · อสนี crit +4/7/12% · ปฐพี HP +30/70/140 · จันทรา skill cooldown -5/9/15% · ทอง coins +10/20/35%. Slots: 1, +1 at Lv 5 / 10 / 15 (max 4). 3 unequipped runes of the same kind + rarity fuse into the next rarity; sell 20 / 80 / 300 coins; bag 40 (full = auto-sell). Runes lie on the ground as glowing diamonds (rarity-coloured halo), magnet like coins, stay 90 s.
- Save `hoodfall1` now also keeps pts / st / bag / eq / rid (max HP is recalculated, not saved). Code: `RT`, `rb()`, `heroAtk / critC / spdMul / cdMul / goldMul / recalc`, `dropRune / gotRune`, `drawMenu`.
- Checked: level up gives points, +1 / +5 buttons, rune pickup, fuse 3 -> rare, equip / replace when slots are full, crit / attack numbers change, panel on 1280x720 and 812x375, no errors.
- Kit sheets 1-6 came back (owner, pasted inline: need them as FILES to cut, e.g. saved into `art/incoming/thai/kit/` on the PC). Owner: "ไม่เอาศาลภูมินะ" = no spirit houses anywhere (removed from the ?map=thai test props and from the kit prompt). Review: platforms / wall / corner, ladders / stairs / vines, gate, prang, broken wall, sala, jars, palms, banana, campfire, pond, graves, rain tree, plinth, root wall, flowers, wisp, 3 background strips = all usable; the "chedi" came out like a bell and the stone lantern looks Japanese (skip or redo).

## Kaiju game: 3D-pixel city test — `kaiju.html` (owner 2026-11-15 "ผมทำเทส 3D พิกเซลก่อน (ฟรี) เป็นเมืองกล่องหนึ่งบล็อก ล็อกกล้องมุมเฉียง")
- Page: `kaiju.html` (three.js r147 vendored in `vendor/threejs/three.min.js`, no CDN). A 4 x 4-block box city (2 park blocks, ~45 buildings, ~140 trees, 18 cars, all made in code: window textures, roofs with helipads / tanks / AC boxes, roads with dashes + zebra crossings, sidewalks, grass). Rendered into a LOW-RES target where 1 render pixel = 1 pixel of the kaiju sprite (U = 24 world units / 204 px), then a post pass adds dark outlines + light top edges from the depth buffer; the browser enlarges it with nearest = chunky pixels. Camera and kaiju snap to the pixel grid (no swimming).
- Camera LOCKED (no rotation), 4 presets on the top-right button: A low 30 degrees, B 45 (default), C high 60, D straight-on 40 (roads square to the screen). Zoom = whole pixel steps (1 / 1.5 / 2 / 3... device px per game pixel; phone default 2). Outlines can be switched off (button / `?ol=0`).
- Play: joystick on the left half (touch) or WASD, เหยียบ = stomp (hop, slam, purple shock rings, damage in 13 units, knocks trees, flings cars), กดค้าง พ่นไฟ = purple breath cone (30 units, sets buildings on fire). Walking into a building pushes / damages it. Buildings shake, shed chunks, then sink into the ground in a dust cloud and leave a rubble pile; % destroyed in the top-left panel. Sounds made in code. Building HP = 16 + volume / 40 (small ones fall to one stomp, mid ones two).
- Kaiju art: the owner's GPT pixel pictures, NOT PixelLab: single side picture `art/incoming/kaiju/owner_kaiju_16bit.jpg` and the 4-view sheet `owner_kaiju_4view.jpg` (right, left, front, back three-quarter), both on a ~3.3 / ~6.85 px grid, snapped with `GPTPX_GRID=3.1,3.6 tools/art/gptpx.py <cell> <out> 14` (cells + results in `art/incoming/kaiju/gpt4view/`), through `pixel_punch.py` -> `art/kaiju/k16_{side,sideL,front,back}.png` (untouched in `art/kaiju/orig/`). The page picks the view from the walking direction on screen: right = side, left = the owner's own left view (not a mirror), toward the camera = front, away = back. `?k=96` = the older 96 px set (`k96_*`: our side downscale + PixelLab front 1).
- PixelLab this session (2 credits, see the job log): front view redrawn from a side picture, twice. Owner on the result: "เหมือนคนละตัวเลย" (looks like another monster) -> not used in the game. Lesson: a PixelLab re-angle of a side picture changes the face; the GPT sheet kept the design. For 8 directions the faithful way: GPT draws the 4 diagonals too (free), or PixelLab `create_character` v3 with reference = the GPT front (south) and `direction_images` = GPT right (east) / left (west) so PixelLab only fills the rest; with these sizes (canvas ~240 x 216) that is ceil(240*216*8/65536) = 7 + 0.25 per import, NOT 4 (the 4 was for a 128 canvas). The GPT views are eye level while the camera looks down 30-60 degrees: reads fine in the test, a top-down-ish redraw is the open question.
- Checks: `node tools/qa/kaiju_play.js <shots> 3` (phone 844x390 at DPR 3, real pointer drags / button presses, every camera preset, stomps, breath, zoom): no errors, sprite picks right / left / front / back correctly, 2 buildings down after 5 stomps; PC 1920x1000 and `?k=96` load clean. Not tried on a real phone (frame time headless = software GL, not meaningful).
- Open for the owner: which camera angle (A / B / C / D); whether to make the 8-direction stills (GPT free vs PixelLab ~7-8 credits); walk / stomp / breath animations; city size and goal (timer, army fighting back, growing bigger).
- Owner "ไม่ใช้ 128 หรือ" -> "เอา 128": the default kaiju is now ~128 px (`art/kaiju/k128_{side,sideL,front,back}.png`, side 128x115, front 102x126, back 112x122), made FREE from the same GPT 4-view sheet by sampling it on a coarser grid (`GPTPX_GRID=6.02,6.1 tools/art/gptpx.py <cell> <out> 14`, each game pixel = the most common colour of its cell, so it stays crisp; raw in `art/incoming/kaiju/gpt4view/*_128.png`, side by side `compare_204_vs_128_x2.png`) + `pixel_punch.py`. In `kaiju.html` the button "อสูร 128 / อสูร 204" switches sets (`?k=128` default, `?k=16` = 204 px); the whole city follows the kaiju pixel (phone: 4 device px per game pixel with 128, 2 with 204). Same city for both (roof textures use their own random). Screenshot `art/incoming/kaiju/city_128_vs_204.png`.
- With a 128 canvas, PixelLab 8 directions (v3, reference = front, our right / left as direction images) would be ceil(128*128*8/65536) = 2 + 0.25 per import, about 3 credits (not 7-8).
- Flames on the back (owner asked "ไฟที่หลังเอาไงเจนมาเลยหรือใส่เอา"): decided WITH the flames drawn on the body (they are the monster's look; keep them in every pose / clip), plus a code layer of small purple embers rising from the drawn flame pixels (the page finds them by colour; more while breathing).
- Owner on the 128 in the game: "ทำไมมันแปลกๆ" / "ขยายหรือ" -> "ไม่ต้องขยาย": it was drawn at the same on-screen size as the 204 set, so each pixel was blown up ~1.6x (phone 4 device px per pixel) and the code-made 128 lost small details (eyes, claws, thick outline). Now every set uses the SAME screen pixel size (phone 2 device px per game pixel, PC 2): the 128 kaiju looks smaller on screen and the view shows more city; + zooms in. A native 128 (GPT free, or PixelLab redraw 1 credit on "เจน") would be crisper than the code-made one.
- Owner confirmed: "เอาเท่า 128 ดังเดิม", "ฉันไม่ได้ต้องการตัวใหญ่มาก", "ตัวเล็กแต่คมโอเค มันจะมีปล่อยตัวอื่นอีก" = keep the kaiju at 128 shown 1:1 (not enlarged), small but crisp is fine, MORE KAIJU will be added later (plan for several kaiju of ~128 px; the 4 x 4 city now fits on one phone screen at the default zoom: a bigger city will be needed). Shot `art/incoming/kaiju/city_128_native.png`.
- GPT prompt for a NATIVE 128 redraw of the 4-view sheet (owner "ไม่ให้gptลดขนาดให้คมก่อนหรือ"): `art/incoming/kaiju/PROMPT_GPT_kaiju_128.txt` (attach `owner_kaiju_4view.jpg`). When it comes back: snap with `tools/art/gptpx.py` (find the grid), punch, replace `art/kaiju/k128_*`.

On the owner's "เจนเลย" the whole sheet went through ONE PixelLab `image_to_pixelart` redraw B at 260x260 (1 credit, job log): a real 128 set, crisper and darker than the code-made one; it replaced `art/kaiju/k128_*` (the code-made ones stay in `art/incoming/kaiju/gpt4view/*_128.png`). Recipe for the next kaiju: GPT 4-view pixel sheet -> one redraw B of the whole sheet at (128 / view width in the sheet) x sheet size -> cut white + fringe -> punch.
- 8 directions IN (`kaiju.html` default `?k=8`, `art/kaiju/k8/<direction>.png`): the page picks the picture from the walking angle on screen (8 sectors of 45 degrees, north = walking away from the camera), no mirroring. Older sets stay reachable with `?k=128` (4 views), `?k=16` (204 px), `?k=96`.
- Owner DIRECTION 2026-11-15: "เกมนี้เป็นเกมสร้างปีศาจละให้มันสู้กัน" = the game is about CREATING monsters (kaiju) and making them FIGHT each other (in the city); also "ปรับยูไอให้สวย", "ทำแมพให้สวย".
- UI + map pass (owner "ปรับยูไอให้สวย", "ทำแมพให้สวย", free): game-style HUD in `kaiju.html` (portrait card with the kaiju face, HP bar, fire-energy bar; city meter "เมืองถล่ม %" with 50 / 75 marks; round icon buttons with drawn SVG icons for camera A-D / zoom / outlines; big skill buttons with footprint / flame icons, stomp cooldown sweep, energy ring round the fire button; combo pop-up "ถล่ม! xN"). Breath now uses fire energy (drains 26/s, refills 7/s and from damage dealt). Map: sea on the two sides nearest the camera (pixel wave shader, foam line, shallow / deep bands), wavy beach, lighthouse (destructible), 5 bobbing boats (sink on stomp / breath / walking into them), the kaiju can wade into the shallows (slower, splashes, ripples), ~190 street lamps (knocked over), neon blade signs + rooftop billboards with made-up letters, roof gardens, park ponds with paths. Shots: run `node tools/qa/kaiju_play.js <dir> 3`.

## Stone-age tribe in 3D pixel — `tribe3d.html` (owner 2026-11-15 "สร้างเผ่าดึกดำบรรพ์ ... ทำ 3D แบบนี้ได้ไหม" -> "ทำเลยแมพกว้างๆ", "3D พิกเซล", free)
- New page, same engine as `kaiju.html` (three.js r147 vendored, low-res render 8 px per world unit, depth outlines, pixel-snapped locked camera with presets A-D, zoom steps). No PixelLab: everything is code.
- Map 360 x 360 units (180 x 180 tiles of 2 units), terraces of 2.2 units from seeded noise (`?seed=N`): mountains + snow to the north, a winding river on the east, a lake on the west, sea + beaches to the south, broadleaf and pine forests (~3.5k trees), rocks, ~14k grass tufts / flowers (instanced), strata on cliff walls, a 64 px detail texture so the ground has pixel speckle. Water: tiles below ground with a wave shader (foam on the shore tiles, shallow / deep by distance to land).
- Village (centre 0, 10, flat radius 40): plaza + paths to each hut, big campfire, totem, 9 huts (cone / grass dome / mammoth-bone dome), longhouse, racks, wood + stone piles, 2 more campfires, palisade ring with an east gate, 3 watchtowers, garden plots outside, a cave in the cliff to the north with two torches, a dock on the river. 16 blocky placeholder villagers wander (real villagers = PixelLab 8-direction sprites later, ask first).
- Controls: drag / WASD to pan, wheel / pinch / Q E / buttons to zoom, camera button (C), time-of-day button (N: day / sunset / night; at night the fires light the village with flickering point lights, smoke darkens). Shadows follow the view in whole shadow texels.
- Check: `node tools/qa/tribe3d_check.js <shots> 3` (phone 844x390 at DPR 3): no errors, build ~0.2 s + load ~3.7 s headless. Shots `art/incoming/tribe3d_{day,wide,night}.png`. Not tried on a real phone: 3.5k trees + 14k tufts + terrain may be heavy on an older phone (lower the counts if it stutters).
- Next ideas (not built): place / build huts on a grid, gathering (wood / stone / food) with the tribe.html rules (villager STR / AGI / INT / VIT), animals (mammoth, dinos) and raids, real villager sprites.
- Beauty pass 1 (owner "สวยกว่านี้ได้ไหม", free): colour grading in the post pass (richer colours, cool shadows / warm lights, depth haze on far land, soft vignette; per time of day), warmer sun + cooler sky fill, drifting cloud shadows on the ground (terrain material patched with onBeforeCompile), grass lips hanging over cliff edges, round low-poly tree crowns (3 blobs) and 3-layer pines, fireflies at night. Before / after `art/incoming/tribe3d_before_after_pretty1.png`.
- Owner "Blender เลยพี่" / "รันในเครื่องหรือคลาวด์": Blender runs IN THE CLOUD session: `pip install bpy` (bpy 5.3.0 for Python 3.13, Blender 5.3 headless, ~400 MB, install once per new session). Plan: model the props (huts, trees, rocks, campfire...) with Blender Python scripts in `tools/blender/`, export .glb with vertex colours to `art/tribe3d/models/`, load them in `tribe3d.html` (GLTFLoader from the three r147 npm package, `examples/js/loaders/GLTFLoader.js`).
- BLENDER IN USE (owner "Blender เลยพี่", free): `tools/blender/tribe_assets.py` (run `pip install bpy` then `python3 tools/blender/tribe_assets.py`) models 11 low-poly flat-shaded props with face colours painted into vertex colours and exports .glb to `art/tribe3d/models/`: tree_broad_0..2 (bent trunk, branch, 4-5 lumpy noise-displaced crowns), tree_pine_0..1 (4 ragged cone layers), rock_0..2 (displaced, flattened, moss on top faces), hut_cone (layered ragged thatch, crossed poles, doorway, hide flap, stone ring), hut_bone (hide dome, 3 mammoth-tusk arches, entrance arch, skull), campfire (stone ring, teepee logs, ash). `tribe3d.html` loads them with three's GLTFLoader (vendored), converts the glTF linear colours back to the painted ones (pow 1/2.2), draws trees / rocks as instanced meshes with a small colour tint per instance; `?blender=0` = the old code shapes (also the fallback). Lessons: a bmesh spin round Y sweeps the circle below the ground with a positive angle (use a negative one) and leaves the tube inside-out (reverse_faces). Shots `art/incoming/tribe3d_blender_{day,night}.png`.
- Owner asked "เราไม่ได้ใช้พิกเซลของ PixelLab แล้วหรือ": map, buildings, trees = code + Blender (free); PixelLab stays for CHARACTERS (villagers, champion, animals) as 8-direction pixel sprites standing in the 3D world like the kaiju.
- Blender batch 2 (owner "ปั้นเลยไม่ต้องรอ", free): hut_dome (layered grass skirts, top knot, door posts), longhouse (log walls, plank ends, ragged thatch roof slabs, ridge pole, crossed gable poles), totem (4 painted carved drums with eyes / beaks, winged top), watchtower (leaning legs, X braces, plank deck, rope rail, layered roof, ladder), rack (forked posts, 3 ragged hides, 2 fish), woodpile (logs with light cut ends, stone axe), stonepile, palisade_log (sharpened tip, rope band; instanced), cave_mouth (rock arch + dark hole, placed against the north cliff, the palisade has a gap there), mammoth (466 faces: fur shades, trunk + tusks as tubes, ears, eyes, tail). 6 mammoths wander the grassland outside the village (stay on their terrace, keep away from water, the village and rocks). Helper `tube()` in the script = bent tapered tubes. Shots `art/incoming/tribe3d_v4_{village,mammoth}.png`.
- Owner 2026-11-15: "สามารถเจนเพิ่มได้เลยนะหากต้องการอะไร" = standing OK to generate when something is needed (still: say what + cost before each job, standard / 1-credit first, never pro first). For the 3D route PixelLab is rarely needed (Blender + free CC0 packs).
- Owner asked whether dropping pixel art is easier: YES for characters / animals (3D models turn to any direction and use ready-made animations; 2D pixel needs every direction x every move drawn or generated). The pixel LOOK is only a render filter: `tribe3d.html` now has a "พิกเซล / เรียบ" button (`?px=0` = smooth: full screen resolution, same view at the same zoom; particles keep their size). Compare `art/incoming/tribe3d_pixel_vs_smooth.png`.
- Free asset plan (owner showed the Quaternius site with its $3 "All in One" support bundle): CC0 packs from Quaternius (nature, animated animals, animated dinosaurs, animated people for villagers), Kenney (Nature Kit, Survival Kit), KayKit (Forest Nature). Stone-age buildings stay Blender-made. Files are not reachable from the cloud yet (hosts blocked): either the owner downloads and uploads the .glb / .gltf files to `art/incoming/<pack>/`, or the session network gets those hosts. Every third-party pack gets a notice in `vendor/<pack>/` (licence + source).
- Self-download test (owner "โหลดเองได้ไหม"; hosts added to the environment on the owner's approval: kenney.nl, quaternius.com, drive.google.com, drive.usercontent.google.com, poly.pizza, static.poly.pizza): quaternius.com pages load (each pack page links a public Google Drive folder: Blends / FBX / OBJ, e.g. dinosaurs = folder 1u5Fhu3ziuRlGonW6bUI7uClqBGoSNeF6 with Apatosaurus, Parasaurolophus, Stegosaurus, Trex, Triceratops, Velociraptor), the folder listing works (`tools/assets/gdrive_fetch.py`), BUT every file download answered Google's "Quota exceeded" page (2026-11-15 ~14:00 UTC): retry later. kenney.nl and poly.pizza answer 403 to the cloud (bot protection). Plan when files arrive: open the .blend (or import the FBX) with bpy in the cloud and export .glb with the animations; licence notice in `vendor/quaternius/`. Fallback: the owner downloads on the PC and a Claude Code session ON the PC copies the glTF / blend files into `art/incoming/quaternius/` and pushes (cloud sessions cannot reach the PC folder yet: the Windows Claude app does not offer it to cloud sessions).
- Quaternius Ultimate Animated Animals IN THE REPO (owner downloaded the zip on the PC, 2026-11-15, free, CC0): 12 animated glTF models in `art/incoming/quaternius/animals/` (Alpaca, Bull, Cow, Deer, Donkey, Fox, Horse, Horse_White, Husky, ShibaInu, Stag, Wolf; embedded buffers, no textures, 12-13 clips each: Attack, Death, Eating, Gallop, Gallop_Jump, Idle, Idle_2, Idle_2_HeadLow, Idle_HitReact1/2, Jump_ToIdle, Walk). Licence + notice `vendor/quaternius/`. Kept as 3D (owner: "ไม่ต้องถ่ายเป็น 2D"); not wired into `tribe3d.html` yet (next: load with the vendored GLTFLoader + AnimationMixer; convert to .glb with bpy if size matters, ~37 MB as .gltf).
- Quaternius Ultimate Animated Animals IN (owner downloaded on the PC, 2026-11-15): 12 self-contained .gltf with 12-13 animations each (the pack has 12 files, Horse_White = second horse colour) in `art/incoming/quaternius/animals/`, licence + list in `vendor/quaternius/`. 3D files kept as 3D (owner: do not render to 2D). Also downloaded on the PC, not yet in the repo: Dinosaur Animated Pack (6, FBX / blend only, no glTF), Ultimate Animated Character Pack (~350 MB unzipped), Ultimate Stylized Nature (~470 MB unzipped).
- QUATERNIUS ANIMALS IN (owner pushed the pack from the PC: `art/incoming/quaternius/animals/*.gltf`, notice `vendor/quaternius/`): 8 stone-age-fitting kinds converted losslessly to GLB with `tools/assets/gltf2glb.py` -> `art/tribe3d/animals/` (Deer, Stag, Horse, Horse_White, Bull, Cow, Wolf, Fox; ~2 MB each, 16 MB; Shiba / Husky / Donkey / Alpaca left out). `tribe3d.html` loads them AFTER the map shows (`?animals=0` off), clones skinned models with `SkeletonUtils` (vendored), swaps the materials to Lambert (same light as the map), scales by height (a bit larger than life so they read: deer 3.1, horse 4, wolf 2.2...), spawns ~37 animals in herds on open meadows (no tree within 4 tiles, away from the village), each with an AnimationMixer: idle / eating / head-low idles, walk, gallop (shy deer gallop more), staying round the herd's home and on their terrace. Clips available: Attack(_Headbutt/_Kick), Death, Eating, Gallop, Gallop_Jump, Idle, Idle_2, Idle_HitReact1/2, Jump_toIdle, Walk -> ready for hunting / fights later. Shot `art/incoming/tribe3d_herds.png`.
- KENNEY SURVIVAL KIT IN (owner uploaded `kenney_survival-kit.zip` straight into the cloud session: uploads land in `/root/.claude/uploads/<session>/`): 17 CC0 GLB + the shared palette `Textures/colormap.png` in `art/tribe3d/kenney/`, notice `vendor/kenney/`. `tribe3d.html` loads them (`?kenney=0` off), merges each model into one geometry, one Lambert material on the palette (linear, nearest), places by target height / length: autumn + tall trees mixed into the broadleaf forests (~20%), fishing stands at the small fires, bedrolls by the huts, wood / stone resource piles, stone grinder, bucket, fish, signpost at the east gate, a logging site of stumps + felled logs outside the fence, a fence round the garden, a hunters' camp (tents, fire, fishing stand, bedrolls) on the river bank by the dock. Shot `art/incoming/tribe3d_kenney_village.png`.
- Quaternius Ultimate Stylized Nature IN THE REPO (owner's zip, 2026-11-15, CC0): 63 models. 36 as glTF in `art/incoming/quaternius/nature/gltf/` (birch, maple, dead trees, bushes, flowers, grass; textured, normal maps re-saved losslessly as RGB, 30 MB folder), the 27 with no glTF in the pack as FBX in `nature/fbx/` (normal / pine / palm trees, rocks, petals, plants; no texture links inside, textures sit in `gltf/`; three's FBXLoader is not vendored yet, or convert them to .glb with bpy). Notice `vendor/quaternius/NOTICE.md`. Not wired into `tribe3d.html` yet. These are textured, stylised (smooth) trees, a different look from our flat-coloured Blender props: compare before swapping.

## TRIBE 3D = THE GAME (owner 2026-11-15 night, before sleeping: "ตื่นมาต้องได้เห็นแมพใหญ่ แล้วก็เกมเพลย์ ขอสวยๆ ... ทำงานต่อไปเองเรื่อยๆ คิดซะว่าตัวเองเป็นเจ้าของเกม ตัดสินใจเองได้เลย")
- Owner's concept (fixed): CITY BUILDING by day, DEFEND THE HOME AT NIGHT, EXPAND COLONIES (outposts) for more resources, to fight enemies that get STRONGER every night. Claude decides the rest as the game owner. Budget: PixelLab up to 200 credits (bosses like a giant bird "หรืออะไร"), no worry about chat quota. Cheapest-route rule still applies: Blender / code / CC0 packs first.
- Design decisions (Claude):
  - Page stays `tribe3d.html`. Map bigger (480 x 480 units), chunked instancing so the trees / rocks outside the view are culled.
  - Resources: wood (trees), stone (rocks), food (berry bushes, hunting herds, farm plots). Population with houses; villagers (Blender cavemen) gather automatically to the nearest store, build, and fight with spears at night.
  - Day 120 s / night 60 s cycle with smooth light change; at dusk a warning, at night waves come from the map edges toward the nearest building.
  - Buildings (cost wood / stone): hut (+4 people), storage pit (gather point), farm (food), watchtower (spear thrower), palisade segment (blocks), outpost totem (claims territory radius 30 around it = colony; only buildable at the edge of owned land), bonfire (light + morale). Place by tapping in build mode (ghost, green / red).
  - Enemies by night n: wolves (Quaternius, Attack / Death clips) -> + bulls -> + foxes swarm -> boss every 5th night: giant bird (Blender, flies over walls), alpha wolf, raging mammoth. HP / count grow per night.
  - Lose: the chief's longhouse destroyed. Score = nights survived (best kept in localStorage). Save of the colony in localStorage.
  - UI: resources + population + day / night dial + night counter on top; build bar at the bottom (cards with icon + cost); messages; PixelLab pixel icons for resources / buildings if they fit (1 credit each, standard).
- Progress (update as batches ship): see the lines below this one.
- Batch 1 (map + speed): map 480 x 480 units (240 x 240 tiles); terrain in 24-tile chunks and every instanced kind in 60-unit chunks with their own bounding spheres (off-screen ones are culled); broadleaf crowns 20-face blobs (96-116 faces a tree); fewer tufts; shadow box +-78 units, shadows redrawn every 2nd frame. One frame: ~700 draw calls / ~1.1 M triangles (was 1371 / 3.6 M).
- Batch 2 (THE GAME, first playable, all free): second `<script>` in `tribe3d.html` = `GAME` (hooks: `GAME.step` from the map's step with the speed factor, `GAME.draw` overlay, `GAME.init` after load; QA helpers `GAME.cheat.res/night/day`, `GAME.spot(type)`, `?autostart=1`). Title screen (start / continue + how-to + best), HUD (wood / stone / food / population, day clock with phase text + bar), build button + build bar with 3D thumbnails rendered by the main renderer, ghost placement (tap ground -> green / red + reason, "สร้างตรงนี้" / "ยกเลิก"), the old time button = game speed x1/x2/x3, HP bars + floating numbers on a 2D overlay canvas, WebAudio horn / build / throw / hit / dawn sounds, autosave at dawn and on build (`localStorage tribe3d_v1`, best nights `tribe3d_best`).
  - Day 110 s + dusk 10 + night 65 + dawn 8, lights blended smoothly from the TIMES presets (sun moves, fires glow at night).
  - Villagers = Blender cavemen (3 poses: stand / step / thrust swapped for walking, chopping, stabbing; per-villager tint): gather wood (trees), stone (rocks), food (berry bushes seeded round each colony, hunting the Quaternius herds which play Death), carry to the nearest store (hall / storage / outposts), jobs split by shares so all three resources come in; births every 22 s when food >= 45 and room; eat pop/3 per 20 s; at night they fight enemies within 26 units with spear thrusts, else gather at the hall; heal by day and near bonfires.
  - The old village is adopted as the start: longhouse = chief's hall (store, 500 HP, destroyed = game over), the 9 huts (2 people each), 3 watchtowers = working spear towers. Territory ring (gold line) radius 60; outposts add radius 34 rings and seed new nodes.
  - Buildings: hut (+4), farm (+0.5 food/s by day), spear tower (range 30, 15 dmg), palisade wall segment (enemies stop and break it), bonfire (light + heals), storage, outpost (must stand at the edge of owned land).
  - Night raids: 2 + 2n + n^2/6 animals (wolves, foxes from night 3, bulls from 4, stags from 6), HP x(1 + .17(n-1)); every 5th night a boss (giant Blender bird that circles and dives, raging mammoth on 10, red alpha wolf on 15...). Enemies walk to the nearest building / villager, use the palisade gates (east, north) in 2 steps, slide along shores, stop and break walls; survivors flee at dawn; food reward at dawn.
  - QA: `node tools/qa/tribe3d_game.js <shots>` (title click, gathering, real clicks: build bar -> hut -> tap -> confirm, a night raid, dawn, a boss night): no errors; one day = wood 110 -> 318, stone 60 -> 184, food 90 -> 157, people 8 -> 12.
- Batch 3: brighter moonlit nights (enemies glow faint red, flash on hits), 5 RESOURCE SITES out in the wild (quarries = big rocks with 160 stone each, berry groves = 70-food bushes) 95-185 units from the village, labelled in the world ("· ตั้งด่านหน้าใกล้ๆ" until an outpost's land covers them) -> the reason to chain outposts (land radius 40, placed within 16 units of the edge); MINIMAP top-left (land colours, gold territory rings, buildings, sites, red enemies, the view box; tap = jump there). Shots in the QA folder: bosses (bird diving, red alpha wolf), minimap.
- Batch 4 (polish): title / continue screen over the PixelLab key art (1 credit, job log; game-over screen stays dark), red / orange arrows on the screen edge toward off-screen enemies (bigger + name for bosses), all 6 building types tested by placement, save -> reload -> "เล่นต่อ (วันที่ N)" restores day, resources, buildings and outposts. Shots `art/incoming/tribe3d/shot_title.png`, `shot_night5.png`.
- Batch 5 (balance, `node tools/qa/tribe3d_balance.js [days]` = a simple player that builds tower / hut / farm / walls / fire / outpost every morning, game at full speed): first run was far too easy (no damage by night 13, 13k of each resource, 80 people) because raids spawned too slowly / too far and many never arrived. Now: the whole raid spawns within ~38 s in packs of 4 from one side, just outside a random piece of our land (outposts get raided too), count 3 + 2n + n^2/4, enemy HP x(1 + .24(n-1)), damage x(1 + .14(n-1)); every extra building of a type costs +15%; population capped at 40 (phones); villagers carry 6 per 3 s trip; farms .3 food/s; eating pop/2 per 20 s; buildings mend 1.2 %/s by day. Result: nights 1-8 comfortable, losses from ~13, the sim player falls on night 14 with 400 kills.
- Batch 6: tap a building (outside build mode) -> panel with level, HP, UPGRADE (to level 3; tower +50% damage, +5 range, faster; hut +3 people; wall +60% HP; farm +60% food; hall +250 HP and +8 land; outpost +10 land; cost wood 40 x level + stone 30 x level) and REPAIR (wood = missing HP / 8); levels saved. First-day tips one at a time (build a tower at the gate, villagers gather by themselves + huts, tap to upgrade / repair, minimap sites + outposts, night arrows). Shot `art/incoming/tribe3d/shot_upgrade.png`.
- Batch 7 (2026-11-16, free): CHIEF'S SKILLS = the player's own part in the night fights (owner's taste: big bright effects aimed at a spot on the ground). Three round buttons on the right above "สร้าง" (keys 1 / 2 / 3): tap a skill -> a ring on the ground (follows the mouse on PC, sits in the middle of the view on touch) -> tap the ground = cast; tap the button again = cancel. ฝนหอก (day 1, 16 s cooldown, radius 8): 18 spears fall from the village side, half of them seek a beast in the ring, each 8 x power; then a ground slam 34 x power on everything in the ring (hits the giant bird too). หม้อไฟ (unlocks day 2, 26 s, radius 7): a clay pot arcs from the hall, explodes 26 x power, then the ground burns 6 s (13 x power per s, beasts slowed to 55%). กลองศึก (unlocks day 3, 50 s): villagers healed, hit x2.5 and faster, towers shoot twice as fast for 12 s, gold aura + ring waves from the hall. Power = 1 + .22 (day - 1) (enemy HP grows .24). Effects = the CC0 PVFX Foundry sheets in `art/pvfx/` drawn 1:1 at 8 px per unit (ground-slam, big-explosion, fire-nova, ground-flames, dust-puff, hit-spark on every hit, power-up-aura), each frame its own quad so no texture copies; gold spark particles, a shared flash light, a small screen shake, noise-made whoosh / boom / drum sounds. Unlock messages at the start of day 2 / 3, a night-1 tip. Fix: the hint box in the middle of the screen no longer eats taps. Checks: `node tools/qa/tribe3d_skills.js <shots>` (real clicks on the buttons and the ground), `SKILLS=1 node tools/qa/tribe3d_balance.js 20` (the sim player also throws each ready skill at the thickest pack). Shots `art/incoming/tribe3d/shot_skill_*.png`.
- MORNING STATE (2026-11-16): `tribe3d.html` is a full game loop. Open questions for the owner: difficulty after playing on the phone, which bosses next (PixelLab budget unused except 1 credit: 199 left of the 200), real villager / enemy art (Quaternius character pack would give animated people), music.
- ASSET WISHLIST for `tribe3d.html` (owner "ขาดแอสเซ็ตอะไร เผื่อลืม", 2026-11-16), free CC0 first, in order of value:
  1. DONE in batch 9 with KayKit (GitHub, CC0). Still open: enemy raiders (a rival tribe) could reuse them recoloured. Was: people with real animations (walk / run / attack / work / death) for villagers + enemy raiders: Quaternius "Ultimate Animated Character Pack" (`quaternius.com/packs/ultimatedanimatedcharacter.html`) or "Universal Base Characters" + "Universal Animation Library"; Kenney "Animated Characters". FBX / glTF / blend all fine (Blender in the cloud converts). Now: Blender cavemen with 3 static poses.
  2. Dinosaurs as enemies / bosses: Quaternius "Animated Dinosaurs" (`/packs/animateddinosaurs.html`: T-rex, raptor, triceratops, stegosaurus, apatosaurus, parasaurolophus; walk / run / attack / death). Drive downloads from the cloud hit "Quota exceeded" on 2026-11-15.
  3. Sounds + music: Kenney audio packs (Impact Sounds, RPG Audio, Interface Sounds), CC0 music (OpenGameArt CC0) as .ogg / .mp3: wolf howl, roar, spear throw / hit, building collapse, horn, footsteps, day + night loops. Now: synthesized beeps. (PixelLab also has create_sound_effect / create_music: costs credits, ask first.)
  4. Extra nature for variety: Kenney Nature Kit. Quaternius Ultimate Stylized Nature is ALREADY in `art/incoming/quaternius/nature/` and not used yet (birch, maple, dead trees, bushes, flowers) -> use it next.
  5. Optional: VFX (hits, blood / dust, fire) = code particles now; the repo already has pixel VFX sheets in `fx/` and `art/pvfx/` (CC0) to wire in; UI icons = drawn SVG now (Kenney Game Icons if wanted).
  How to bring them: attach the zip in the cloud chat (uploads land in the session) or push from the PC into `art/incoming/<pack>/` + licence in `vendor/<pack>/`.
- Batch 8 (owner 2026-11-16 "งานที่ทำตอนนี้คือปรับกราฟิกละกัน", free): Quaternius Ultimate Stylized Nature in the map + a big speed fix.
- Batch 9 (2026-11-16, owner "ทตัวเข้าเกใส์" = put characters into the game; free): REAL ANIMATED PEOPLE. Quaternius' Google Drive answers "Quota exceeded" to every download from the cloud (itch.io is not reachable either), so the people come from the CC0 KayKit Adventurers pack on GitHub (vendor/kaykit/): the Barbarian (bear-head hood, fur cape, axe) = men, the Rogue recoloured to leather / ochre / rust = women, and the chief = a bigger Barbarian with the two-handed axe and round shield standing at the hall. Files `art/tribe3d/people/Barbarian.glb` (13 of the 76 moves kept: idle, walk, run, chop, slice, two-hand chop, pick up, interact, throw, hit, death, cheer, sit) + `Rogue.glb` (no moves of her own, same rig) = 1 MB in all (was 7.2), `tex_*.png` colour variants; tools `tools/art/kaykit_trim.mjs` (gltf-transform), `tools/art/kaykit_recolor.py`. In the game every body is merged into ONE skinned mesh (axe / hood / cape become bone-weighted) = one draw call per person. Moves: run when moving, chop at trees and rocks, pick up at bushes, throw when a hunt lands, slice / chop in night fights, hit, death, everybody cheers at dawn and on the war drum; the chief turns and throws when a skill is cast, cheers at dawn. LESSON: textures for this scene must be LinearEncoding (the scene's colours are plain vertex / material colours); sRGB-decoded textures came out nearly black. Old Blender cavemen stay as the fallback while the people load. Check `node tools/qa/tribe3d_people.js <shots>`. Shot `art/incoming/tribe3d/shot_people.png`.
- Batch 10 (2026-11-16, owner: the game "ยังแปลก" because "หมู่บ้านเสร็จแล้วตั้งแต่เริ่ม" + "ไม่รู้ต้องทำอะไร"; wants "สร้างฐาน + ป้องกัน", "ผสมระหว่างทาวเวอร์กับสร้างเมือง"; free): BASE BUILDER + TOWER DEFENCE.
  - Start from scratch: only the chief's hall (longhouse), the big fire, the cave and the dock; 4 villagers, wood 120 / stone 60 / food 80, land radius 50. The old hand-made village shows only with `?village=full` (`PALI` = palisade gates logic only then). Save key `tribe3d_v2` (v1 saves had the old village).
  - Hall level 1-5 = the progression: caps per type `CAP` (hut 2/4/6/8/10, farm 1-5, tower 1/3/5/7/9, wall 8-40, fire 1-4, store 1-4, outpost 0/1/2/3/4), upgrade costs `HALL_UP` (100/50, 220/140, 400/280, 650/450 wood/stone), each +250 HP, +2 people, +8 land; the panel lists what the next level unlocks. Other buildings level up to 3 but never above the hall. The hall shoots: the chief at its door throws spears (range 24, 9 dmg).
  - Construction: a placed building is a site (low, gold progress bar); the 2 nearest villagers drop their work and come to hammer (`recruit`), idle ones join (max 2 per site); seconds of work `BTIME` (hut 10, farm 8, tower 14, wall 3, fire 5, store 7, outpost 18); a site gives no room / food / land / shots until done.
  - Tower defence: each day the next wave is planned (`planWave`): count (night 1 = 3 wolves, then 1 + 2n + n^2/4, bosses every 5th night) and 1 / 2 / 3 spawn points (nights 1-3 / 4-7 / 8+) just outside all our land; shown all day as red marching chevrons on the ground from a red war banner to the hall, "ศัตรูจะมาทางนี้" labels / edge arrows, and the clock text "คืนนี้: หมาป่า 3 · อีก 84 วิ". Button "เรียกศัตรูมาเลย" starts the dusk now, bonus wood .35 per second skipped (+ half as stone). Raids spawn at the banners.
  - Quests (13, panel under the minimap, tap = go do it: opens the build card / selects the hall): hut, tower near the red arrows, farm, survive night 1 (try the spear rain), hall 2, 4 walls, 8 people, outpost, survive night 5, hall 3, 4 towers, survive night 10, hall 5; each pays wood / stone / food. The build button glows while the quest wants a building; the card has a green outline. Day-1 tips trimmed to two.
  - Checks: `node tools/qa/tribe3d_base.js <shots>` (real taps: quest -> hut -> villagers build it in < 18 s, wave preview, call early, night from the banners, hall upgrade by tapping the hall, save -> continue). Balance sim now upgrades the hall first each morning.
  - SPEED BUG FOUND: three r147 sets `frustumCulled = false` on every InstancedMesh, so the 60-unit chunks of batch 1 were never culled (the whole map was drawn every frame: ~2 M triangles + the shadow pass). `inst()` now sets `frustumCulled = true`. The shadow box also follows the zoom now (half size = view footprint + 10, 30..78 units, was a fixed +-78), so fewer things go into the shadow map and shadows are crisper.
  - `tools/blender/nature_pack.py` (Blender in the cloud): imports the chosen Quaternius glTF / FBX, bark decimated to 12-25 %, 40 % of the leaf cards dropped and the rest grown x1.18, base on the ground, exported without images -> `art/tribe3d/nature/*.glb` (35 models, 1.9 MB) + 64-256 px textures in `tex/`. Licence: `vendor/quaternius/`.
  - Look: leaves use the texture only for the cut-out shape (alphaTest .32) and get a FLAT colour per species (maple orange, birch yellow, green, teal pine, bush green, `NAT_FLAT`), crown normals point out from the crown's middle (soft ball shading) -> clean pixel-art trees instead of speckle (the raw textures looked muddy, maples dark red). Leaf shadows use a cut-out depth material.
  - Where: every lone meadow tree, the forest edge (1 tile) and 6 % autumn / birch accents inside forests are Quaternius (2261 trees); the deep forest keeps the light Blender crowns. Kenney autumn trees are no longer used when the pack loads. Ground: the box tufts became grass clumps (x2-2.4) + 5 kinds of flower clumps; 1241 bushes (6 kinds, some with flowers) on meadows and forest floors; dead trees on rocky ground. Ferns (Plant_*) dropped: their flower texture made them blue.
  - Numbers (headless, default zoom): main view + shadow pass ~1.0-1.7 M triangles a frame (the old map without Quaternius: ~2 M main + shadow every frame). `?qn=0` turns the pack off. `TB.cam` exposed for QA.
  - QA: `node tools/qa/tribe3d_game.js` no errors (raid, boss night). Shots `art/incoming/tribe3d/shot_nature_before_after.png` (left before, right after), `shot_nature_meadow.png`.

  - New building ป้อมทหาร (fort: wood 60, stone 30, 14 s to build, HP 260, cap 1/1/2/2/3 by hall level; a ring of sharpened posts, totem, red banner). Tap it: "ฝึกทหาร +1" / "ฝึกให้เต็ม" (food 15 + wood 5 each, 6 s each, paid when queued); room 4 per fort, +2 per fort level (fort levels need the hall level). HUD counter with a red shield = soldiers / room.
  - Soldiers (`SOL`: 60 HP, 10 dmg every .9 s, speed 6.2; +25% per fort level, +10% per night; war drum x2.5) = the chief's body (two-handed axe + round shield) in moss cloth. By day they stand round their fort facing out; whenever a beast is within 60 of the fort or 18 of our land they run out by themselves and fight, then go back. Beasts like to target them (they are the front line). They eat like villagers.
  - Villagers are workers only now: at night they walk to the big fire and sit round it; they hit back only at a beast within 6.
  - No loot: beasts no longer drop food and the dawn food bonus is gone; food / wood / stone come only from gathering, hunting herds, farms (plus quest rewards and the call-early bonus).

Comes every night from night 3 except boss nights (last in the wave list; clock text "ยักษ์ป่า Lv.N + ..."). Never killed: at 0 HP it limps back to the forest (`giantBeat`++). `giantLv` +1 after every night it came, +.25 for every building it smashes; HP 260 x (1 + .45 (lv - 1)), damage 20 x (1 + .2 (lv - 1)). Saved (`gl`, `gb`).
  - Walls in 3 tiers: รั้วไม้แหลม 160 HP -> กำแพงหิน 420 (wood 10 + stone 25, needs hall 2) -> กำแพงหินเสริม 900 with spikes (stone 60, hall 3); the mesh changes (grey blocks + cap, then taller darker blocks + bone spikes). Wall panel: "อัปเกรดทุกช่วง xN" upgrades every wall of that tier while resources last.

## 2026-11-16: map 1 of the grinding game as a 3D-pixel test (`map3d.html`, free, no PixelLab)
Owner (consulting about `map.html`, the map Fable 5.2 repainted on 2026-11-13): "หากจะทำแมพให้เป็น 3D พิกเซลละ" -> "ตัวละครจะมีปัญหาไหม" -> "ลองทำดู1แมพ".
Agreed in talk first: same characters, nothing generated; the camera stays locked (only front / side / back pictures); 4 risks named: pixel sharpness, sprites sinking into walls, flat sprites lit wrong, camera angle.
- Data: `EXPORT3D=<tmp> python3 tools/art/td_map.py` (new switch: writes only the painter's masks + a ground painted without the tree / bush shadows; normal output checked byte-identical) then `python3 tools/art/map3d_build.py <tmp>` -> `art/td/map1/3d/`: `top.png` / `top3d.png` = the ground seen from straight above (plateau tops moved back down by 62 px; ground hidden behind a plateau = the ground north of it mirrored), `grid.png` (footprint / walkable / water), `geo.json` (2 stairs, 75 props, 13 monster spawns, start, all in world map px).
- Page `map3d.html` (three.js r147 like kaiju / tribe3d): plateaus are real blocks 94 units high (= the 2D wall height seen from camera A), each wall quad takes the painted wall right above it in `ground.png`, stairs = 8 real steps out from the wall, sun shadows, depth outlines (not on the flat pictures: they write alpha 0 and the outline pass skips them).
- Sharpness rule: 1 render pixel = 1 pixel of the swordsman sheets (U = 0.5 map px); map pictures 2:1; every card snapped to the pixel grid; camera A = 48.6° so a ground art pixel is exactly 4 x 3 render pixels. Default zoom puts ~780 render rows on screen (hero ~17%); ซูม +/- = 1, 1.5, 2, 3, 4 device px a pixel.
- Everything flat (hero, monsters, rocks, ruins, bushes, trees) stands upright facing the camera, stretched 1/cos(pitch) so it shows at its true pixel size; lily pads lie flat. Pictures 60+ px tall write depth, so the hero behind a rock / wall / 3D tree shows as a violet see-through outline. Pixels under the feet are pulled toward the camera so they never sink into the ground.
- Buttons: มุม A 49° / B 60° / C 35° / D turned 22° (walls and the side of rocks show), ต้นไม้ รูปเดิม (the map's own tree pictures + painted shadows) / 3D (Quaternius trees and bushes from `art/tribe3d/nature`, real shadows, pine darker than in tribe3d), เวลา กลางวัน / เย็น / กลางคืน (cards tinted by the time). Keys WASD, C, Q/E, T, N, O. URL `?cam= ?z= ?trees=3d ?t= ?ol=0`.
- Walk only: the swordsman walks (run side / up / down clips, idle clip, front / back stills, like map.html), climbs the stairs, cannot step off a cliff; 13 monsters wander round their camps. No combat, no other maps. `map.html` untouched.
- Checked (Playwright, 844x390 DSF 2 and 1920x1000): no page errors or failed requests; stairs walk 0 -> 35 -> 94; finger drag moves him; all 4 buttons; hidden behind the big rock -> violet outline. Not tried on a real phone (speed unknown: render ~1.3 M pixels + shadows). Shots `art/incoming/map3d/shots.jpg` (A with the map's own trees / D turned with 3D trees on a PC / evening).
- Open for the owner: which tree look, which camera, zoom; then whether to move combat / levels / the other two maps over.

## 2026-11-16: tribe3d — Ultimate Fantasy RTS buildings + Ultimate Monsters (owner downloaded wishlist v2 items 1-2)
Owner: "ใส่เข้าเกม tribe3d.html: 1. Ultimate Fantasy RTS = อาคารเปลี่ยนหน้าตาตามขั้น 2. Ultimate Monsters = ศัตรูกลางคืนกับบอสแบบใหม่ 3. เสียง Kenney + เพลง". Free, no PixelLab.
- Buildings (`rtsOf()` / `RTS_SC` in `tribe3d.html`, models `art/tribe3d/rts/`): hut = Houses_FirstAge_1/2/3 (variant from position) Level 1-3, tower = WatchTower, fort = Barracks, store = Storage, farm = Farm + Wheat, outpost = Archery (+ campfire in front), all FirstAge Level 1-3. Hall: lv1 stays the hand-made longhouse, lv2-4 = Temple_FirstAge_Level1-3, lv5 = Wonder_FirstAge_Level2 (wooden fortress). Every upgrade swaps the model (one scale per type, so higher levels really grow). Walls / fire unchanged (walls already had 3 code-made tiers). `?rts=0` = old code shapes.
- Monsters (`EN` entries with `m:`, loaded after the map shows; if not loaded a wolf comes instead; `?mon=0` off): tribal "นักรบเผ่าป่าเถื่อน" (night 2+), mush "เห็ดพิษ" (3+), ghost "ผีป่า" flying (4+), orc "อสูรออร์ค" (5+), mixed in with the wolves / foxes / bulls / stags. Bosses every 5th night now rotate: นกยักษ์ (5), ราชาเห็ด (10), แมมมอธคลั่ง (15), เยติยักษ์ (20), มังกรไฟ flying (25), จ่าฝูงหมาป่า (30), ปีศาจรัตติกาล (35), then round again (`BOSSES`). The forest giant still comes on the other nights.
- Screenshots: `art/incoming/tribe3d_rts_buildings.jpg`, `art/incoming/tribe3d_monsters_night.jpg`.
- NOT done: item 3 (Kenney audio + music) — no audio zip was on the PC; the game still uses the code beeps (`sfx()`). Item 5 (Dinosaur Animated Pack zip is in Downloads, FBX / Blend only) not used yet.
- Same day, item 3 done (owner: "ให้ Claude โหลดเอง"): Kenney Impact Sounds / RPG Audio / Interface Sounds / Music Jingles downloaded from kenney.nl on the PC + 3 CC0 loops from OpenGameArt (list + sources: `vendor/kenney-audio/NOTICE.md`). `sfx()` in `tribe3d.html` now plays real samples (table `SFX`: hit / bhit (beast hits a building) / shot (spear lands) / throw / build / drum / boom (+ synth rumble) / horn (prehistoric drum sting at raid start) / click (buttons) / dawn / lose / boss jingles), random variant + small pitch change, minimum gap per sound; the old code beeps only play while a file is still loading. Music: day loop by day, battle loop at night, cross-faded with the sky (`musicStep()`). Speaker button top right = all sound on / off (saved in `tribe3d_snd`), `?snd=0`. QA: `GAME.snd()`. Jingles were picked by a pitch analysis (rising = dawn, falling = defeat), not by ear: swap names in `SFX` if the owner dislikes one. Other OpenGameArt CC0 candidates heard of: "Tribal" by Of Far Different Nature (3 min, 7.7 MB mp3, not stored).

The Tools moves would make villagers chop / pickaxe / hammer for real (not wired yet).

Lookbook `tools/art/archer_lookbook.js` (attach offsets in its CFG; Blender +Y ends up as glTF -Z, so non-bow pieces get rotateX(90°)); pictures `art/incoming/tribe3d/archer2_closeup.png`, `archer2_compare.png`.   1. Graphics modes on the old พิกเซล / เรียบ button, now 3 steps พิกเซล -> เรียบ -> สวย (`GFX` 0 / 1 / 2, saved in `tribe3d_gfx`, `?gfx=N`). Default: a PC (fine pointer, window >= 1000 wide) starts in สวย, phones in พิกเซล. สวย = up to 2x supersampled smooth render (browser smooths it down; capped at 8.3 M px), PCF soft shadows (materials recompiled once on the switch), a rim back light (`RIM`, always in the scene at 0 so the light count never changes), smooth unit / monster textures (textures marked `userData.unit`).

- Same day, owner "ปรับกราฟิกโดยรวม" (free): looked at day / dusk / night, PC + phone (`node tools/qa/tribe3d_look.js <folder> [pc|phone]` builds a small base and shoots each), fixed what stood out:
  - Dusk turned all dirt neon orange: softer golden hour (sky 0xe8d0c8, sun 0xffcc9a .52, sat .94, warm [1.07, .99, .9], fire .35); campfire light 0xffa860, range 46, decay 1.6.
  - Night: buildings were black blobs: brighter moonlit night (hemi .62 sky 0x7484c8 ground 0x3a3a5c, moon .4 0xb4c6ff).
  - Ground: in the fresh-start game the old footpaths to the deleted hand-made huts were still painted: now a round dirt plaza (radius ~13-16) + rare worn patches (`FRESH0` in the terrain kinds; `?village=full` keeps the old paths).
