  - LESSON (twice in one batch): a `// comment` added in the middle of a one-line JS statement silently comments out the rest of the line (the big campfire light vanished, the carry line broke): use `/* */` mid-line.
  - Before / after: `art/incoming/tribe3d/gfx_before_after.png`.

## 2026-11-16 night: tribe3d becomes a FASTER TOWER DEFENCE + new upgrade panel + ★★ units (owner before sleeping: "มันไม่สนุกเลย ui ที่อัพเกรดทำให้มันดีๆ", "ปรับรูปแบบการเล่น", answers: not fun because "กลางวันรอนาน ไม่มีอะไรทำ", direction "ทาวเวอร์ดีเฟนส์ล้วน เร็วขึ้น"; then "ทำตัวเทียร์ตัวอื่นๆ ด้วย", "นอนแล้วหวังว่าตื่นมาจะได้เล่น")
- Flow: day / night = build phase / wave. `DAY 40, DUSK 4, NIGHT 30 (held while beasts remain, at most WAVE_MAX 120 s; ends as soon as the wave is cleared), DAWN 4`; a new game starts at `G.t = -FIRST` (35) so the first build phase is 75 s. HUD: "คลื่นที่ N", "คลื่นที่ N: หมาป่า 3 · อีก 30 วิ", "กำลังรบ · ศัตรูเหลือ N", "ชนะคลื่นนี้แล้ว!"; the red button is "▶ เริ่มคลื่นเลย (ไม้ +N)" (wood .3 per skipped second). Night / day wording -> waves everywhere (quests, title, game over "ผ่านมาได้ N คลื่น").
- No waiting: villagers keep gathering / building during a wave and run to the big fire only when a beast is within 16 (`v.hide`); farms and births run all the time; gathering faster (carry 10, work 2.4 s), farms .5 food/s, start wood 200 / stone 120 / food 120; the giant from wave 4.
- Lanes: walking enemies head for the HALL and only take on a building within 9, a soldier near them or a villager within 5 (retarget every .8 s); walls block; flyers keep the old nearest-target rule. Tower placement along the red arrows now matters.
- Building panel (`select()` + `statsOf()`): picture of the building at its level (`thumb(t, lv)`, cached), name, stars ★★☆ (hall 5, others 3), HP bar, a stats table "now ➜ next" (tower damage / range / shots per s, hall spear / homes / shamans, hut homes, farm food per s, fort soldiers / tier, outpost land), unlock chips with pictures for the hall, cost chips (red when short), a big green "อัปเกรด ➜ ขั้น N" button (grey "ทรัพยากรไม่พอ" + shake when short), repair, wall upgrade-all, ✕. Short screens: 2-column stats, smaller pictures, scrolls. Upgrading: gold rings, sparks, aura, the building pops up into its new look, "ขั้น N! ★★".
- Range ring: light-blue ring of the reach while placing a tower or with a tower / the hall selected.

## 2026-11-17: the owner's red-scarf hero in 3D (owner sent a design picture after "ทำยูนิคใหม่ได้ไหม ... หรือต้องการแบบ")
- Picture kept as `art/incoming/tribe3d/hero_ref.png` (chibi swordsman: spiky reddish-brown hair, red headband + red scarf, purple tunic with gold bands, grey shoulder pads, brown boots, dark broad sword with red runes). Made for free, no PixelLab: KayKit 2.0 Knight body (helmet, visor, cape off; its own young face) + new colours + Blender pieces.
- Body: `tools/art/hero_build.mjs` (run like kaykit2_build.mjs, from the gltf-transform scratch folder) writes `art/tribe3d/people/Hero.glb`: the Knight's parts get their UVs moved per part / per swatch (and by height: legs below 0.27 = boots, arms past |x| 0.78 = bare fists) to new swatches painted into the UNUSED bottom half of the Knight atlas by `tools/art/hero_tex.py` -> `tex_hero_0.png` (purple / dark purple / brown boots / gold / dark belt / reddish-brown hair / skin; each keeps the shading profile of the swatch it came from). The chest rosette is gone (purple), the buckle is gold.
- Pieces: `tools/blender/hero_gear.py` (loads Knight.glb and casts rays at its head / body so things sit on the surface; game coordinates through `G(x, y, z)`) -> `art/tribe3d/gear/hero_gear.glb`: `HeroHair` (25 fat, flattened, curved clumps, smooth-shaded, 3 browns), `HeroBand` (headband round the forehead line + knot + 2 tails), `HeroScarf` (roll round the neck, front knot + drape, 2 torn tails blown back to his left), `HeroPad` (steel dome + second plate + gold rims), `HeroBadge` (gold chevron + red gem on the chest), `HeroPouch`, `RuneBlade` (hand-slot space like the KayKit swords: dark core, light edges, gold guard with a red gem, red glowing chevrons / diamond on both faces, glowing tip edge). Hero gear is drawn double-sided, glow* unlit.
- In the game: template `PEOPLE.tpl.hero`; it is now the tribe CHIEF (stands by the hall, throws for the skills, cheers after a wave); `tribe3d.html?chief=old` brings back the crimson barbarian chief. Not a trainable unit (yet).
- Lookbook `node tools/art/hero_lookbook.js out.png` (design picture + front / side / back / swing) -> `art/incoming/tribe3d/hero_look.png`. Known differences from the picture: KayKit face (smaller eyes, no anime brows), the sword is held level in the KayKit idle (the picture holds it down), scarf tails are rigid.
- Same day, owner on that first version: "ไม่เหมือนเลย". Cause: the KayKit Knight's own face, head shape and boxy armour body. REDONE FROM SCRATCH: `tools/blender/hero_model.py` -> `art/tribe3d/people/hero_model.glb` (330 KB): the whole hero modelled after the picture: round chibi skull, decal face laid on the skull by ray casts (big eyes with whites / iris / pupil / glint, thick dark angry lids, slanted thick brows, tiny nose, small frown), ears; hair = a full shell + 25 big smooth leaf-like clumps (bangs from the top front hanging OVER the headband down to the brows, locks before the ears, crown tufts sweeping up and back, side clumps hanging out and down, back layers); headband on the forehead skin; puffy two-roll scarf with the knot and two torn sagging tails behind his left shoulder; slim purple tunic with a lighter front panel and gold chevron, belt + gold buckle, split front flaps with gold edges, pouch; layered faceted steel pads with gold rims; purple sleeves, big gauntlets with two gold bands, bare fists; dark purple trousers, big gold knee bands, brown boots with cuffs and grey toe caps; head x1.08. Colours muted to the picture (slate purple, orange gold, brown-red hair).
- How it plays: every part is named `<bone>|<part>` and built in the BIND POSE of the KayKit Rig_Medium (head pivot 1.24, shoulders y 1.11, hips 0.52 ...). The game clones the bare Knight skeleton, drops its skinned body, pins each part to its bone and merges everything into ONE skinned mesh with vertex colours (`mergePerson(root, keep, mat, skel)` now takes a skeleton and a `vertexColors` material), so all KayKit moves play with one draw call. `RuneBlade` (no bone prefix) = the sword gear, turned in the right hand slot by Euler (-2.125, -0.589, -2.622) so the KayKit idle holds it low to his right with the flat side up like the picture (computed from the slot's idle orientation).
- The first try's files (Hero.glb, tex_hero_0.png, hero_gear.glb, hero_gear.py, hero_build.mjs, hero_tex.py) are deleted (git history). Lookbook `tools/art/hero_lookbook.js out.png [yaw]` now shows the picture, the same 3/4 view big, then side / back / swing -> `art/incoming/tribe3d/hero_look.png`.
- RECIPE for any new unique unit from a picture: copy `hero_model.py`, keep the bone layout, model the parts; a 3/4 render next to the picture after every change (most of the likeness is face decals + hair volume + colours).
- Same day, owner "แก้สวยกว่านี้": hair = sharp anime spikes (long taper, hooked tip, 10-sided, profile .85 -> .02) swept up and BACK (no sideways mane), wider bangs; shoulder pads = three layered steel plates (flat lids) with gold rims and a gold ridge, pre-tilted .28 rad so the idle arm (~50 deg down) carries them over the shoulder; winged gold chest emblem; wavy scarf tails. Soft shading: Cycles bakes ambient occlusion into a vertex colour (`AO`, distance .32) at the end of `hero_model.py`, exported with `export_vertex_color='ACTIVE'`; `mergePerson` multiplies each part's colour by `AO_LO + (1 - AO_LO) * ao` (AO_LO .5). The hero's merged material is `MeshStandardMaterial` (roughness .62) for soft highlights.

## 2026-11-17: the owner's Tripo AI model = the ★★★ archer (owner sent `tribal_female_warrior_3d_model1.glb` from the Tripo app)
- The file: one static fused mesh (93k tris, 75k verts split at texture seams into 3,025 islands), 4096 colour / normal / metal-roughness maps, 11.6 MB, 0.97 tall, facing +z, A-pose with the bow in her LEFT hand. Kept raw in `art/incoming/tribe3d/tripo/warrior_raw.glb`; licence note `vendor/tripo/NOTICE.md` (owner to check the Tripo plan: the repo is public).
- Rig (free, Blender in the cloud): `python3 tools/blender/tripo_rig.py <raw.glb> art/tribe3d/people/warrior_f.glb`:
  - joins the seam-split vertices (UVs stay per corner), decimates to 40 %, works at x10 (Blender heat weighting FAILS on models under 1 unit, LESSON), and builds an armature named like the KayKit Rig_Medium at her joints (read off grid renders, table `J` in the script).
  - Heat weights also fail on Tripo's self-crossing mesh, so they are solved on a voxel-remeshed PROXY (voxel .06 at x10) and copied over with a Data Transfer (nearest face). That proxy only solves when made from the 40 % mesh; the 24k-face game budget comes from a second decimate after weighting.
  - Fixes by whole texture islands, decided by where they sit and their texture colour: the bow + the blue string go fully to the left hand (rigid); the feathers and gold discs hanging off the hood go to the head; braids and cape behind the arms go to the head / chest. An arm or leg bone only keeps weights inside a capsule round its own joints (arm .055, leg .075), so the cape flap by her right hand and the skirt edges stay with the body.
  - Result: textures 1024 JPEG, no metal map (metal reads black without an environment map), 24k tris, 1.9 MB; her joints go into the glTF extras.
- In the game, `rigTripo()` in `loadPeople`:
  - clones the bare KayKit Knight skeleton, puts every bone on her joint and turns it from its KayKit rest by the rotation that takes the KayKit bone direction to hers. Her A-pose limbs are therefore "unbent" to KayKit's T-pose inside the bind, and every KayKit clip plays unchanged.
  - Her clips drop the bone translation / scale tracks (her bone lengths stay) and keep the hips move scaled to her hip height (`T.clips`, used by `newPerson`, portraits and lookbooks).
  - She uses her own MeshStandardMaterial (map + normal map, metalness 0).
  - She is `PEOPLE.tpl.hunter3` = นักธนู ★★★ (fort level 3); she plays the bow idle / draw / release moves with her own bow.
- Checks: `node tools/art/unit_lookbook.js out.png hunter3 "Idle:.3,Ranged_Bow_Draw:.8"` (any template, any moves), `node tools/qa/tripo_weights.js out.png handl,lowerarml,...` (weights by colour). Tiers / units / people checks clean; three ★★★ huntresses fight 6 wolves with no errors.
- Picture for the owner: `art/incoming/tribe3d/warrior_f_look.png`.
- The same pipeline takes any Tripo / AI character. For a new one, re-read her joints off a grid render, then check the bow / cape / braid island rules: they are written for this model.

## 2026-11-17: ★★★ archer = KayKit body + the Tripo hood and bow (owner "เอานักธนู KayKit มาทับได้ไหม" -> chose "ผสมกัน")
- Why: the whole Tripo huntress moved, but her skirt stretched with the legs when running, the cape / braids were rigid and the bow draw looked weak. The KayKit Ranger body moves cleanly in every clip.
- `python3 tools/blender/tripo_parts.py art/tribe3d/people/warrior_f.glb art/tribe3d/gear/huntress_parts.glb` (700 KB) cuts her into `TripoHood` (skull-fur hood, horns, gold discs, red feathers; her face, brown hair, braids, scarf and collar dropped by position + bone weight + texture colour) and `TripoBow` (bow + string: what follows her left hand, minus the hand). Her joints go into the extras.
- In the game, `tripoParts(T, gl, kscene, body)`: each piece is read in the frame of HER bone (`tripoSkeleton`, the bone re-posing taken out of `rigTripo`), scaled, put in the frame of the same bone of the KayKit body and skinned rigidly to it as a SECOND skinned mesh with her texture (`userData.extra`; `newPerson` skips it when it picks the body mesh). Fit table `TRIPO_FIT`: hood on `head` x.93 of (Ranger head width x1.12 / 2.6), offset (0, -.07, .1); bow on `handl` x1.45 of the body ratio.
- `tpl.hunter3` = Ranger + `range_1` (red scarf) + hood + bow (no Antlers / Mantle / KayKit bow). `?archer=tripo` = the whole Tripo huntress as before (`warrior_f.glb` is only loaded then).
- Checks: units / tiers / people clean, portrait OK, both versions load with no errors. Clip for the owner: `art/incoming/tribe3d/archer_mix_moves.mp4` (not in git, mp4 ignored). Known: the KayKit hair shows under the hood front (like her bangs), the bow tip touches the ground in the idle side view.
- Same day, owner "แก้ท่าที่ไม่สวย": (1) the bow took its angle from HER hand, so in KayKit's idle / walk it dragged slanted into the ground and stood straight up on the dead body. Now `bowFrame()` finds each bow's long axis (PCA), string side and grip, and the Tripo bow is turned onto the KayKit `bow_withString` frame in `handslotl` (grip on grip, `TRIPO_FIT` k = 1.12 x the KayKit bow length): idle / walk = held level at the hip, draw = upright with the string to the body, like the KayKit archer. (2) KayKit's own Death_A also leaves the bow standing up (★★ too): bow units (`hunter2`, `hunter3`) get their own clip list `bowDie()` where `handslotl` turns -90 deg about x between .15 s and .6 s, so the bow lies flat along the body. Known: the back of the hood dips a little under the ground when lying dead.

- Moves (all KayKit, already in `hero_anims.glb`): idle `Melee_2H_Idle`, run `Running_A`, combo Slice -> Chop -> Spin, กระโดดฟาด = `Melee_1H_Attack_Jump_Chop`, พายุดาบ = `Melee_2H_Attack_Spinning` (loop). Move gallery lists the sword moves.
- Numbers in ONE table `SWD` (combo-designer rules): per blow clip, time scale, hit time, chain-from, end, damage x attack (1 / 1.4 / 3), reach, arc (160 / 90 / 360 deg), lunge, knock-back, hit-stop (0.05 / 0.07 / 0.12 s, global `HS` slows game time to 12 %), shake. Hit times = where the sword hand moves fastest in each clip (measured), divided by the time scale. Input buffer 0.28 s, the next press within 0.6 s goes on with the combo, a blow's recovery cancels into a dodge, the hero walks up to a clicked / nearest target (up to 13 m) before swinging and never lunges into it.

Cooldowns 6 / 7 s.
- Swoosh effect: an arc strip built in code (`arcGeo`, texture `SLASH`) revealed along the swing then fading; additive white + orange glow over a deep red-orange normal-blend layer (additive alone vanished on the pale earth); side slash flat at chest height, chop vertical overhead -> front + ground ring / dust, spin a full ring.
- Check: `node tools/qa/grind3d_sword.js <out> wing|demon` (combo on dummies 4 swings / 5 hits, slam, whirl, dodge, 28 s auto fight: Lv 3, 16-19 kills, no errors); archer check unchanged and clean.

## 2026-11-18: `grind3d.html` becomes a real grinding game (owner: "ทำเกมส์เก็บเวล 3 D")
- 4 zones (`ZONES`): 1 ทุ่งเห็ด Lv 1 (mushroom, wolf; boss ราชาเห็ด), 2 ป่าเงา Lv 6 (wolf, ghost, tribal; boss หัวหน้าออร์ค), 3 ค่ายออร์ค Lv 12 (orc, tribal, demon; boss จอมปีศาจ), 4 ภูเขาน้ำแข็ง Lv 18 (yeti, ghost, demon; boss มังกรน้ำแข็ง). Same clearing, each zone recolours sky / fog / ground / leaves; monsters (Quaternius CC0 already in `art/tribe3d`) load per zone.
- Monsters come in packs of 2-4 (up to 9 alive, `?mon=`), level = zone Lv + 0-3 (life +22 % / damage +16 % / XP +30 % per level), 14 % of packs have a gold "หัวหน้า" elite (1.3x size, 3x life, more drops). Monster Lv shown over the life bar.
- Zone bar (top middle): 25 kills (`QUOTA`) call the zone boss (zone Lv + 4, boss life bar at the top). Boss skills = RED GROUND WARNINGS (`warnZone`: red rim, fill grows until it hits): a big circle on you, a ring around the boss, or 5 small circles in a row; bosses are not stunned and barely pushed. Beating a boss opens the next zone (button "แดน" left, or N); the last zone's boss comes back for farming.
- Gold coins from every kill fly to the hero -> shop (button "ร้านค้า" or B, the game pauses): attack +8 %, life +10 %, crit +2 %, gold +15 % per step, cost x1.3-1.45 per step (`UPS`).
- Saved in the browser per hero (`grind3d_v1_<hero>`): level, XP, gold, shop steps, open zones, current zone. `?save=0` = no save, `?reset=1` = start over. Training dummies only on `?dummy=1` (the old checks use it).
- Check: `node tools/qa/grind3d_loop.js <out> [hero]` (bot plays zone 1: boss came at ~22 s game time after 25 kills, beaten at Lv 9; shop, zones 2-4 + their bosses, save after reload; no errors). Bot pace is faster than a person: tune `QUOTA` / XP if the owner finds it too quick.
- Open: own map per zone (now one clearing, recoloured), more skills / skill upgrades, gear drops.

Greedy bot: forest archer with skills win 17 lives, without skills win 17 (lost 5 at wave 10, repaired), snow winged knight win 17. All towers maxed by wave 14 (late gold piles up: a sink beyond castle repair is open).
- Draw load (main pass, desktop): forest 186 calls / 200k triangles, snow 179 / 139k.
- Level 4 "ขั้นสุดยอด" for every tower (owner: develop on; also a gold sink for the late game): หอธนู 260 = shoots 2 targets, หอเวท 300 = the bolt chains to 2 more, หอระเบิด 320 = 3 cluster bursts, ลานอัศวิน 280 = 4 knights + the spin stuns; gold spikes, a floating gem and a gold ring at the base; the tower menu shows the trick. Taps on the ground now happen on release (a drag or a pinch does not build / cast); buttons and menus still react at once (`onUI`).
- ENDLESS on one map (owner 2026-11-19: "ตอนนี้ทำแมพเดียวก่อน และมอนมาเรื่อยๆ จะได้ไม่ต้องไปสร้างใหม่"): only ป่าตะวันรอน (the map row is hidden: `?maps=1` shows it, `?map=` still opens autumn / snow), no last wave (`NWAVE` = 1e9), a boss every 5th wave taking the 4 bosses in turn (life after wave 20 = the wave's life x 0.12), the gold goblin on every wave ending in 3 / 8, 12 s between waves (was 25), the wave bonus stops growing after wave 30. The game ends when the castle falls: "ไปได้ถึงคลื่นที่ N", the best wave per hero (`guardtd_best_<map>_<hero>`) on the hero cards. Greedy bot with skills: falls at wave 35 (castle repairs from wave 29).
- BUG fixed (live for a few minutes in the zoom update): a `//` comment ended the line holding `const shake`, so anything that shakes the screen (bombs, bosses, leaks) threw an error. Rule for this file: never end a one-line block with a `//` comment; use `/* */`.
- Arrows "ยิงไม่ตรง" (owner 2026-11-19): monster cards face the camera and lean back with it, but shots aimed straight above the feet (they landed on the legs / the ground in front). Shots, damage numbers and life bars now use the card as seen (`monPt`: the feet + the camera's up x height); splash still counts round the feet; arrows made smaller (they read as spears).
- Before every guardtd ship: `node tools/qa/guardtd_alltowers.js <hero>` for each hero (all towers at level 1 and 4, all skills, 6 waves) — the owner hit a freeze with the bomb tower after a ship without this.
- MAZE mode, now the default (owner 2026-11-19 "ทำแมพให้สวยกว่านี้ อาจได้เล่นคล้ายๆ green td Dota"): an open grass field of 15 x 8 tiles (painted checker + grid + edge stones), towers on ANY free tile, monsters come out of a purple portal in the rock arch (left) and find the shortest way to the castle gate (right) round the towers (`flow()` = Dijkstra over the tiles, 8 ways, no cutting round a tower's corner; `mazeStep`). A tower can never close the way: `canBuild` refuses a tile that would cut the portal or any monster off, or that a monster stands on / walks to. New tower "กำแพงหิน" 10 gold = a cheap blocker for mazing (all walls are one instanced mesh); tap a wall to build a real tower ON it (its price is added, selling gives back 60% of both). Glowing arrows on the ground show the monsters' way; with the build menu open on a free tile they show the way as it would be (blue) or turn red ("ตรงนี้สร้างไม่ได้: จะปิดทางมอน"). Fliers ignore the maze and fly the straight line (only archer / mage towers and ranged heroes reach them). Hero dais fixed at tile (8,4). Monster life in this mode grows from x1 (waves 1-5, while the maze is still being built) to x1.5 by wave 20 (`HPK(n)`; a built maze makes the walk much longer); a boss roar stuns at most the 4 nearest towers (a maze packs towers close). A safety net (`mzWatch`) re-routes a walking monster that has not moved for 6 s (logged in `GAME.WDLOG`; never fired in the bot games). Zig-zag maze bot (`guardtd_mazebal.js`, 24 towers on 4 wall lines): archer alive at wave 40, mage hood falls at 37, demon at 35 (earlier balance), wing + demon alive at wave 30 with the final numbers; a player who does not maze (`naive=1`) falls around wave 15. Best wave is kept per mode (`guardtd_bestmz_<map>_<hero>`). Title has two pills: "เขาวงกต" / "ทางเดิน"; `?mode=road` = the old road with build pads (unchanged). Tower meshes are now merged per material (`mergeStatic`: ~25 meshes -> ~8 per tower) because a maze game has many more towers. Checks: `tools/qa/guardtd_maze.js` (screenshots + "random walls never close the way"), `tools/qa/guardtd_mazebal.js [hero] [naive]` (zig-zag maze bot / a player who does not maze), `guardtd_alltowers.js <hero> [maze|road]`.

- Gold sinks (owner 2026-11-19 "ตัวละครมันอัพจนตันแล้ว ไม่มีอะไรให้ใช้เงิน"), all modes: a tower at the top level takes STARS for ever ("เพิ่มดาว ★N" on the upgrade button: each star x1.2 damage, price = top-level cost x0.6 x1.5^stars, "★N" floats over the tower, selling gives back 60% of all of it); the hero keeps levelling past Lv 12 with no cap (skill points stop at 12 when every skill is full; each level +10% damage as before; past 12 a level needs +4 x (lv-12)^2 more XP: with a flat curve the bot hit Lv 99 by wave 30); new "ฝึกพลัง" button next to the hero's XP bar = train with gold (each step x1.15 damage, price 150 x1.45^steps). The circle bot spends on stars / training, cheapest first, once every tile has a top-level tower: archer reached wave 35 (was 23), gold stays low (100-300) instead of piling up.

## NEXT GAME IDEA (owner 2026-11-19 night: "เกมไม่สนุกเลย ทำเกมอะไรดี") - a survivor-like ("ไอ้เกม vampire"), 3D
- Proposed, not started: Vampire-Survivors-style horde game in 3D, the guardtd look: one 3D hero (archer / wing / demon / dark / hood
  from guardtd: Tripo models on the KayKit skeleton + KayKit / Quaternius UAL moves) on an open ground, camera tilted and following;
  hordes of our pixel monster cards (art/action/mobs, ~40 kinds + 4 bosses); one thumb to move, attacks fire by themselves;
  XP gems -> level up every 20-30 s -> pick 1 of 3 upgrades (more arrows, orbiting fire, chain lightning, ...; two maxed cards
  combine into an ultimate); runs of 10-15 min, coins after death for permanent upgrades; our additive VFX sheets (art/guardtd/fx,
  fx/) for the skills. Hundreds of monsters on phones need ONE instanced mesh per monster kind (sprite-sheet cell per instance in
  the shader) instead of one mesh + material each as in guardtd. Start in a NEW chat ("ทำเกมเอาตัวรอดจากฝูงมอน ใช้ฮีโร่กับมอนจาก guardtd").

## 2026-11-19 night: NEW GAME `horde.html` "ฝ่าฝูงอสูร" = 3D horde survival (owner: "ทำเกมเอาตัวรอดจากฝูงมอน 3D ใช้ฮีโร่กับมอนจาก guardtd")
- Owner's answers before the build: everything fires by itself ("ยิงเองทั้งหมด"), a run = 10 minutes with a boss at the end, all 5 heroes at once. Then: "ขอแมพสวยๆ เน้นเล่นยาว มีทางเลือกหลากหลาย", "มีอะไรให้เลือกเยอะๆ", "ui ทำให้เหมือนเกมที่เขาขายกัน", and the Monster Catalog (`art/incoming/monster_catalog/`, 288 monsters, 2x sheets) "เผื่อใช้".
- Free: no PixelLab / GPT job. Art = the guardtd heroes, the guardtd monster sheets, the owner's Monster Catalog, Quaternius nature, code-built props, the Dreamina clips of guardtd.
- Monsters: `tools/art/horde_mobs.py` cuts the chosen catalogue monsters (walk + attack, facing left, foot point) into grid sheets `art/horde/mobs/<id>.png` (255-colour palette, hard alpha, transparent = black so the shader divides by alpha: 125 monsters = 20.7 MB instead of 63) and writes ONE `art/horde/mobs/index.json` that also lists the 43 older sheets of `art/action/mobs`. To add monsters: put ids in `PICK` in that tool, run it, add them to a map's `mobs` in `horde.html`. The meadow set of the catalogue copies the roster of a well-known online RPG (pink blob, moon rabbit, drummer cricket): only the plain animals of it are used (larva, beetle, hopper, moth), not the blobs.
- Drawing a crowd: one instanced draw call per monster kind (`loadKind`, `fillBatches`: position / scale, frame cell, mirror, white flash, tint per instance; cards face the camera), one for all shadows, one for every glow (sparks, orbs, bullets, gem shine, pollen / fireflies / embers / snow), instanced gems / coins / arrows. Monster cap 270 (phones 200). Scenery in 24-unit chunks with their own bounding spheres.
- Heroes (guardtd's 5, Tripo on the KayKit skeleton): archer (start: ศรล่า, crit), winged knight (ดาบแสง, armour + regen), demon (พายุดาบ, +15% damage), dark knight (คลื่นดาบมืด, area + life steal), hood mage (ลูกไฟเวท, cooldown + xp). Moves on two layers (legs / upper body, `chPlay`) so they attack while running.
- 15 weapons (levels 1-6, numbers in `WEAPONS[k].s`): arrow, slash, spin, wave, fire, orb, bolt, rain, frost, holy, meteor, blade, aura, poison, spirit. 14 passives (`PASSIVES`). 6 + 6 slots. A level-6 weapon whose partner passive (`need`) is owned can be AWAKENED (`evo`: x1.6 damage, faster, bigger, +1) from a card or a chest.
- A run (`updDirector`): tiers change every 2:30, the mix every 30 s (3 kinds of the tier + 1 older), ring swarm at 1:30 / 4:00 / 6:30 / 9:00, an elite with a chest every minute, bosses at 2:30 / 5:00 / 7:30 (red ground circles, charge, bullet ring, summons), the last boss at 10:00 = win, then "เล่นต่อไม่มีจบ". Pickups: xp gems, coins, heart, magnet, bomb, chest.
- 4 maps (`MAPS`, `?map=forest|grave|volcano|snow`; the next opens after surviving 5:00 on the one before, `?unlock=1` opens all): ground = one seamless tile painted in code x a soft whole-world picture, a tree wall round a 30-unit arena, props with collision. Difficulty x1 / 1.3 / 1.65 / 2 (life; damage and coins follow).
- Long play: coins after a run (picked up + time / kills / bosses / win bonus) buy 15 permanent upgrades (`SHOP`, save `horde_save1`), best time per map and hero.
- Balance (bot `?bot=1`, logic only): forest, 5 heroes: 4 of 5 reach the last boss at level 27-34 with ~3,400 kills; the winged knight won at 11:19 (level 56). The bot is a poor player (it died 3 times before 5:00 in 8 games), a person collects gems better. `HPT(t)` = monster life, spawn rate in `updDirector`, boss life `BOSSHP`.
- Checks: `node tools/qa/horde_check.js look <out> <hero> <map>` (9 screenshots, `?god=1`), `node tools/qa/horde_check.js bot <hero> <map> <seconds>`. Real clicks through every screen were tested once by hand (hero pick, shop buy, start, drag / keys, card, pause, quit).
- Links: `horde.html` (title), `horde.html?map=grave&hero=wing&unlock=1`.
- Open: phone speed is not measured (cloud has no GPU: ~380 draw calls / 550k triangles on the forest title before the chunk change), no hero attack clips for weapons other than the hero's own, pvfx pack not used yet, achievements / hero mastery / map difficulty levels are ideas for more long play.
- Batch 2 (same night, owner asleep, "ทำไปเรื่อยๆ"): more to choose and longer play. 5 difficulty levels per map (`DIFFS`: ง่าย / ปกติ / ยาก / โหด / นรก = life, damage, spawn rate, coins; the next opens when the one before is won on that map; stars on the map card = highest level won; new players start on ง่าย), hero mastery (`SAVE.hx`, xp from every run, 20 levels, +2% damage and life each), 32 missions that pay coins once (`MISSIONS`, button "ภารกิจ" with a red count), shrines on the map every ~70 s (`SHTYPE`: +50% damage 20 s / heal half / coins + magnet / freeze 5 s; stand on it 2 s), arrows at the screen edge to shrines, chests and the boss, zoom (wheel / pinch, kept in `horde_zoom`), graphics quality that steps down by itself under 26 fps (`QL`: smaller render size, then no shadows; `?q=0..3` fixes it), denser early spawns, softer first boss, fewer and smaller trees inside the arena.
- Bot on ง่าย (forest): winged knight and hood mage reach the last boss taking under 60 damage, the archer bot still died at 3:33; other maps ran with no errors (grave: dark knight to the last boss; volcano: winged knight 7:57; snow: archer 5:55).
- Batch 3 (same night): PVFX Foundry pixel effects (`art/pvfx`, CC0) are now used: `pfx(name, x, z, size)` shows a sheet as a picture facing the camera (explosions on fireball / meteor / boss death, lightning strike, frost nova, holy smite, poison cloud, whirlwind on the spin, level-up, heal, power-up aura, ground slam of bosses, arcane burst when an elite dies; at most 22 at once). Monster roles from the catalogue (`role` in `index.json`): ranged / caster kinds keep 4.5-6.5 units away and shoot a slow red bullet every ~3 s, swift kinds +18% speed, tanks +40% life. Blessings: one of three random ones before every run (`RELICS`, 12 kinds, some with a price: glass sword, greed bag, challenge flag). Monster picture book on the title ("สมุดภาพมอน", `SAVE.mk` = kills per kind, 150 kinds by map, unseen ones are silhouettes) + 2 missions for it. Bigger crowds (spawn rate x1.4, cap 320 / phones 230, xp per kill grows slower): bot on ง่าย kills ~6,270 in 10 minutes (was ~4,570), peak 166 alive.
- Batch 4 (2026-11-20, owner: "เอาสวยๆ สนุกๆ นะ"): looked at real mid-game pictures first, then fixed what was ugly.
  - Effects no longer white out the screen: on the bright maps (forest, snow: `BRIGHT`) ground decals use normal blending with deeper colours (`decMat`), big runes / flashes / the Dreamina sheets are fainter, light beams fade toward the top (`BEAM`) instead of solid white slabs.
  - Ground repainted: soft patches + fine mottle instead of round blobs, flowers in patches, a worn ring path + 3 spokes painted into the big picture (`PATHS`, `pathDist`; rocks and props keep off them = lanes to run along), dark soft screen corners.
  - Places on the map: 3 pools inside the ring (water / swamp / lava / ice, a small noise shader, nobody walks through, fliers do) and 5 set pieces outside it per map (`sites`: camp with pixel campfire flames and code-built tents, totem circle, temple ruin, watchtower yard, mausoleum, fenced graves, pillar circle, bone hut, obsidian ring, crystal circle...). Kept LOW (under ~3 units): a tall thing hides the fight behind it. Rune decals on the ground, warm glows.
  - The hero shows through whatever stands in front (a flat silhouette drawn with the depth test reversed, before the hero in the solid pass; off on the low quality steps). A small life bar under the hero when hurt.
  - Monsters (walkers) and dropped things now respect obstacles (`OGRID`, `pushOut`): they slide round rocks, so rocks are cover.
  - Speed: this three.js (r147) turns frustum culling OFF for InstancedMesh; every scenery chunk now has its own sphere and culling ON, the ring wood is in 12-unit chunks, and the far plane stops just behind the visible ground (`CAM.far`): forest in play 357 draw calls / 520k triangles -> ~136 / 227k.
  - Fun: kill combo counter (kills within 2.2 s; milestones 25 / 50 / 100 ... give xp, the end pays a few coins), the gold mimic (a chest that runs away every ~95 s: coins fall out while it is hit, a pile + maybe a chest when it dies, gone after 17 s), boss entrance (short slow motion, the camera leans to the boss, a roar), fanfare on level-up / chest, footstep dust, dust puff when a big monster dies, notes moved to small pills at the left (they covered the middle).
  - Checks after this batch: all 4 maps 10-minute bot games with no errors, click test through every screen, pictures of set pieces and pools on all 4 maps.
- Batch 5 (2026-11-20): minimap bottom-right (ring path, pools, the crowd as red dots, shrines / chests / mimic / boss, the hero as an arrow), swift monster kinds lunge (0.45 s blink, then a straight fast dash: step aside), gems tilted and shinier, confetti on the win screen, softer low-life red.
- Batch 6 (2026-11-20, owner asleep, "เอาสวยๆสนุกๆ"): weapon effects redone after looking at mid-game pictures (a 64 px poison sheet was stretched over 8 units = a wall of blocks; sword cuts were flat pink rings).
  - `decal(kind, x, z, size, colour, hot colour, o)`: flat quads painted by small shaders (`DEC_KIND`), meshes kept and reused (`DEC_POOL`, `freeDecal`): `swoosh` (a crescent whose bright front runs along the arc and leaves a thinning tail: slash, spin, and the flying sword wave with `full: 1`), `pool` (wobbly edge, swirls, bright rim, popping bubbles: poison), `shock` (torn ring + flash: every `boom`, frost, the bomb), `aura` (ring of flames: fire aura), `target` (aim circle with turning dashes; `full: 1` = a ring closing in: meteor). Colours given to `decal` are the real ones (converted to linear; the plain materials of this page come out lighter than their number). Pools and aim circles are never additive, and the rain / frost pictures get fainter as the circle grows: five awakened arrow rains had turned the whole screen white.
  - BUG fixed: `arcFx` drew the sword cut a quarter turn away from where it hit (plane +x vs `dirTo` angles); now `rot = ang - PI / 2`, checked with a monster below / right / above the hero.
  - Pixel effect sheets are never drawn bigger than `PXMAX` = .036 units per pixel (monsters: .013); big areas are shown by decals, with small pixel puffs inside (poison clouds at 1.5 units). Fireball explosions play only the first 60% of their sheet (`pfc`: flash + fire; the long smoke tail looked like glitches when ten go off at once), the meteor plays it all.
  - Weapons that hardly hit (a normal bot game: blade 1k and orb 1-16k damage next to 200-900k for the others): the blade now flies 5.4 units out (was 2.7) before it turns back; the orb ring breathes between 0.85x and 1.75x its radius with bigger balls and a short tail.
  - Checks: `tools/qa/horde_check.js bot <hero> <map> <seconds> <difficulty 1-5>` (new last argument). On ปกติ the winged knight's bot reaches the last boss on all 4 maps (level 43-62, ~7,000 kills).
  - Start of a run on hard maps: a normal-level bot game with the bow hero on the volcano was level 6 after three minutes (119 kills, then dead): one arrow of 10 against 30-45 life. Now the map / level life multiplier comes in over the first two minutes (`HPT`: 30% of the extra at 0 s, all of it from ~120 s; easy levels unchanged) and the arrow goes through 2 monsters from level 1 (pierce 2 / 2 / 3 / 3 / 4 / 6). After that, ปกติ bot games: archer and hood reach the last boss on forest / graveyard / volcano (level 41-54), the archer still dies on snow at 5:36; wing / demon / dark reach it on every map tried. The bot has NO shop upgrades and plays badly, so ปกติ may now be easy for a person: watch the owner's word, ยาก / โหด / นรก are there.
  - Dash (`heroDash`, `DASH` = 3 s cooldown, 0.2 s at speed 15 = 3 units, nothing hurts the hero for 0.34 s): the only thing the player does besides walking, attacks stay automatic as the owner chose; added on Claude's own call so the bosses' red circles and the lunging monsters can be answered. Space / Shift on a PC; on a phone a round button left of the minimap (navy disc, three chevrons, a pie while it recharges). The second thumb pressing a button while the first steers used to start a pinch zoom: `pointerdown` now tries the buttons first. KayKit `Dodge_Forward` on the legs. The bot dashes when hurt and crowded. If the owner does not want a second control, removing the button and the key leaves everything else as it was.

The gauge = the ring round the portrait: +1/80 per second and +1/(150 + t x 1.2) per kill, a run starts at 40% (first one after ~20 s), about 14-20 per 10-minute bot game. It fires when it is full and 6 monsters are within 6.5 units (or an elite / boss within 8); the name of the move slides in under the hero, 0.3 s of slow motion, nothing hurts the hero for 1.2 s, and the level-up cards wait 2.9 s (`G.ultHold`) so the move is seen. Damage base `UB()` = 13 x (1 + .45 m + .11 m^2), about 1.9 x a medium monster's life of that minute on ปกติ, times the hero's damage bonus; bosses take half. Area grows with the size passive (`UA`, at most x1.35).
    - archer ห่าฝนศรสวรรค์: 34 arrow strikes over 2.4 s in a 7-unit circle (70% aimed at monsters) on the owner's green Dreamina swirl `art/guardtd/fx/arrow_storm.png`;
    - wing ปีกแห่งพิพากษา: three light waves (push back + 1.2 s stun), a light pillar on each of the 12 toughest monsters within 10, heals 25%;
    - demon พายุดาบโลหิต: 2.6 s spinning storm (the `chak` decal, red, 5 units) that drags the horde in from 8.5 units, nothing hurts him meanwhile, a fire blast at the end;
    - dark ห้วงเงากลืนกิน: a black hole (dark `pool` decal + violet ring) on the middle of the nearest 14 monsters pulls everything in for 2.2 s, then bursts, heals 15%;
    - hood ฝนอุกกาบาตเพลิง: 8 meteors on the crowd (the meteor zones, `w: 'ult'`), the last one giant.
    - Also: the hero's card on the title shows the move (on a low screen the two stat bars share one line to make room), a row with its damage on the pause / result list, two missions (`SAVE.ults`), the first-time note "เกจไม้ตายเต็ม". Bot report prints `ultimates N` and `ult:` in the damage line. In bot games it is 1-9% of all damage (weapons kill almost everything first): it is there for the show and for clearing a crowd, not as the main damage.
  - New decal kind `chak` (a spinning disc of three curved blades): the wind chakram weapon (it was a flat white five-sided ring, `BLADEM`, removed) and the demon's storm.
  - Damage numbers: bold with a dark edge, they pop, rise and slow down, drift a little sideways, fade only at the very end; crits bigger in gold; thousands as 12K.
  - Tree crowns: seen from above every leaf card faces up and took the same light = one flat orange blob at the arena edge. The leaf shader now makes lighter / darker clumps by place and gets lighter toward the top (`natMat`). Camp tents: darker canvas + a coloured cap (they burned out to white under effects).

Not checked: phone speed with five big moves, how the slow motion feels.
- Batch 8 (2026-11-20, owner: "ปรับเอฟเฟคสกิลหน่อยยังดูไม่สวยเพิ่มลูกเล่นไปอีก"): the five ultimates redone for looks.
  - Cut-in: the screen dims round the hero for 0.8 s, then a slanted band in the hero's colour slides across the lower part of the screen with speed lines, the hero's badge, the hero's name and the move's name (it used to be a plain strip of text).
  - New decal kinds: `sigil` (a magic circle that draws itself round: rings, a band of runes, a six-point star, small orbs, outer and inner parts turn opposite ways) under every move, and `wings` (feathered wings that open, flutter and fade; stood up toward the camera with the new `rx` option of `decal()`) behind the winged knight. `decal(..., { force: true })` = never dropped when the screen is busy.

## WAR = `war.html` "ศึกแม่ทัพ": army tactics auto-battler (owner 2026-11-20, latest)
- Owner sent a picture of a grid battle game (squads on tiles, generals in big art) and said "อยากได้วางแผนแบบนี้ ให้จัดยูนิต แล้วปล่อยมันสู้ และมีแม่ทัพ". Genre only: no name / art / maps copied from that game. Zero credits: everything is art already in the repo.
- Board 10 x 6 tiles (`TS` 1.3). The player fills the left 4 columns (blue), the enemy stands on the right 4 (red, visible while placing so you can counter it). Drag cards from the bottom (squads) or the left column (generals) onto blue tiles; drag a placed squad to move / swap, drag it off the board to remove; tap a squad for its info; "จัดอัตโนมัติ" places for you; the last formation is remembered (`SAVE.form`).
- Squad = 1 tile, 1-3 soldiers on one life bar (soldiers fall as it drops, a healer can stand them back up). 8 kinds (`TYPES`): ทหารโล่ (tank, arrows do half), ทหารหอก (x2.2 vs cavalry), นักธนู, ทหารม้า (fast, first hit x2.2 + stun, x1.6 vs back row), นักเวท (fireball splash), นักรบขวาน (cleave), หมอยา (heals), นักฆ่า (leaps behind the enemy at the start, x1.5 vs back row). Each costs command points (`cpMax(s)` = 6 + 1.05 per stage).
- Generals (`GEN`) = the 5 horde / guardtd heroes on the board as a strong unit, an aura for the whole army and a big skill (gauge fills with time and damage; button bottom-left, "ออโต้" casts by itself): ปีกพิทักษ์ (shields + heal all), ห่าฝนศร, ฝนอุกกาบาต, พายุดาบโลหิต (leap + spin), ห้วงเงา (pull + blast). Slots: 1, 2 after 4 stages, 3 after 12. Cut-in band on cast.
- Campaign (`CHAPTERS`): 4 chapters x 5 stages = forest, snow, volcano, graveyard (looks from horde.html; a chapter on another map reloads the page with `?map=&st=&go=prep`). Stage 5 of each = boss: Quaternius Orc / Yeti / Demon / Ghost (CC0) with a red-circle ground slam. Enemy armies are made from roles (front / back / special) per stage, same every time (`enemyPlan(s)`). Unlocks by stages won (`UNLOCK`, `GEN.need`).
- Stars: win, 50% soldiers alive, 80% alive. Gold: win 50 + 20 x stage (first clear x2), a loss pays a little. Camp (ค่ายทหาร): level up each kind and each general (+13% / +10% a level, max 15; `upCost`).
- Looks: soldiers = KayKit Adventurers with their pieces merged into one skinned mesh (`prepBody`), team colour by a hue swap in the shader (cloth -> blue / red, skin and leather kept; healers teal / amber) + a coloured disc under every squad; cavalry = Quaternius horse + knight; fight camera follows the squads still standing. Speed x1 / x2 / x3, pause, retreat.
- Balance (bot `node tools/qa/war_check.js camp 6`, auto formation, upgrades spread evenly): stages 1-19 won first try with stars dropping toward the end, bosses ~45-55 s, the last boss needs replays for gold. Normal fights 15-25 s at x1 (before the x1.35 life change; now a bit longer).
- Checks: `node tools/qa/war_check.js look <outPrefix> [query]` (title, map, camp, prep, fight, end pictures + errors), `camp [tries]`. Untested: phone speed with ~60 soldiers, how it feels to drag on a phone.
- Decision talk 2026-11-20 (owner, consulting, nothing built yet): keep the 3D map with ONE locked diagonal camera; soldiers may become PixelLab pixel cards drawn from that one diagonal view (flipped left / right, like the horde monsters), while the GENERALS STAY 3D ("แม่ทัพใช้3 d"). Suggested: bosses stay 3D too. Before any job: one standard 1-credit test (ทหารโล่) placed on the 3D board, only on the owner's "เทสต์" / "เจน". Estimate for the 8 soldier kinds, one direction: ~5-6 credits each = ~40-50. PixelLab balance checked 2026-11-20: 1,153 of 2,000 left, resets 2026-12-08.
